RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [175] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MEDIAREF，一个公开可用的网络文档知识库，旨在支持对媒体背景核查（MBC）生成进行可复现、低成本评估，覆盖200个媒体来源，并验证其能提升MBC生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的自动事实核查系统假设检索到的证据是可靠的，但现实中信息可能冲突、过时或源自不可靠/有偏见的来源。虽然已有工作提出通过媒体背景核查（MBC）来评估信源可信度，但生成MBC依赖昂贵的商业搜索API，限制了可复现性。因此需要一种低成本、可复现的替代方案。

Method: 作者构建了MEDIAREF——一个覆盖200个媒体来源的公开网络文档知识库，并描述了一套可复现的构建与更新方法。在该知识库基础上，对主流LLM在MBC生成任务上的表现进行评估，并通过自动评估和定性评估两种方式验证MEDIAREF对MBC生成质量的提升效果。

Result: MEDIAREF能够支持更高质量的MBC生成，这一点通过自动评估和定性评估均得到了验证。同时，该知识库实现了低成本、可复现的MBC评估，降低了对商业搜索API的依赖。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF为源批判性推理（source-critical reasoning）领域提供了一个可复现且低成本的公共资源，有助于推动自动事实核查系统中信源可信度评估的研究进展。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [176] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了2010-2026年NLP研究的发表场所变化，发现LLM兴起后，研究人员正从传统*ACL主会轨道向Findings轨道和通用ML会议迁移，且通用ML会议提供显著的引用溢价，推动了这一趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的兴起模糊了NLP与通用机器学习之间的学科边界，研究者观察到NLP论文越来越多地出现在ML会议上，但缺乏系统的量化证据来验证这一学科重心是否正在转移。

Method: 采用两阶段分析：(1) 对已成名作者，比较LLM时代前后在*ACL主会轨道、Findings轨道和通用ML会议上的发表份额变化；(2) 对至少发表3篇一作NLP论文的新晋作者，追踪其2019-2024年发表场所的分布变化；最后使用因果推断方法估算通用ML会议带来的引用溢价。

Result: 成名作者在*ACL主会轨道份额下降19.2个百分点，Findings轨道上升14.8个百分点，通用ML会议上升8.6个百分点（已控制领域并行增长）。新晋作者中，主要发表在*ACL会议的比例从2019年的84%降至2024年的74%，而主要发表在通用ML会议的比例从5%升至21%。通用ML会议存在显著的引用溢价。

Conclusion: NLP研究的发表场所正经历显著迁移，从传统*ACL会议向通用ML会议和*ACL Findings轨道转移，引用溢价是驱动这一变化的重要因素。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [177] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文主张语言模型衡量文化并非被动记录，而是通过"施为切割"主动参与构建文化现实。作者借Barad的新物质主义框架，以影视对话案例展示测量装置自身如何纠缠于文化材料之中，并据此提出理论与实践并重的研究纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP文化测量研究普遍将语言模型视为客观记录工具，忽视了测量装置本身如何参与构成其所测量的文化现实。作者旨在揭示这一认识论盲区，为文化计算提供更具反思性的理论基础。

Method: 以Karen Barad的"施为切割"（agential cut）概念为分析框架，将NLP文化测量的全流程——模型、数据、标注、评估——视为一种"物质-话语实践"。通过三则电视电影对话案例（结构测量、互动测量、偏差测量）和三则装置自身检视（文化标记擦除、历史材料调谐、智能体工作流中的能动性），具体展示装置设计选择如何划定现象与工具的边界。

Result: 分析表明：测量装置并非外在于文化现象，语言模型早已内化了其所测量的文化材料，因此每一次测量都是一次"施为切割"——在方法论与伦理层面同时做出承诺的边界划定。三个案例分别揭示了装置在结构、互动与偏差测量中如何主动形塑文化现实；三种装置检视则进一步暴露了文化标记的系统性擦除、对历史材料的差异化调谐，以及智能体工作流中涌现的装置自身能动性。

Conclusion: 应建立一种理论驱动、经验严谨且文化条件化的研究纲领，将每一次"施为切割"视为自觉的方法论与伦理承诺，而非技术中立的操作步骤。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [178] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过缩放定律研究LLM计算规模与社会模拟保真度之间的关系，发现多数行为与观点模拟任务随规模显著提升，但纵向预测、低资源领域以及人类认知偏差的校准方面扩展效果不佳，表明规模化是必要但不充分的改进路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM社会模拟虽具前景但保真度不足。研究者希望探究：语言模型的缩放范式能否弥合这些差距，还是模拟保真度与通用能力正交、需要独立研究方向。

Method: 使用85个基于Qwen3架构、在DCLM语料上以固定计算预算（10^18至10^20 FLOPs）预训练的Transformer LLM，结合35个更大规模的开源模型（最高70B参数），在三个子领域（观点建模、行为模拟、纵向预测）中应用缩放定律分析计算规模、通用基准成绩与社会模拟保真度之间的关系。

Result: （1）三个子领域均呈现强计算缩放效应；（2）行为和观点模拟任务随规模迅速改进，尤其在英文网络语料中代表性好的人群上；（3）纵向预测和代表性不足的观点扩展较慢，尤其与MMLU等通用推理基准相关性低时；（4）行为模拟中，风险厌恶等认知偏差的校准、以及从相关任务学习关联奖励的人类启发式并未随规模改进，即使微调模型从0.5B到8B也未见明显提升。

Conclusion: 规模化将在大多数场景下改善社会模拟，但存在离群情况，低资源领域的改进可靠性较低。缩小差距需要超越单纯扩展模型规模的研究努力。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [179] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地利用计算手段研究有声书 narration 的声学特征（音色、语速、响度等）与消费数据之间的关系，发现声学信息与吸引力之间存在稳健关联，且该关联在控制标题效应后依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旁白/朗读（narration）是有声书体验的核心，但目前缺乏系统性的计算研究来量化 narration 质量如何影响听众的消费行为。研究者希望填补这一空白，为有声书个性化和 narrat 选角提供数据驱动的洞见。

Method: 从 LibriVox 平台提取有声书的声学特征（使用预训练音频模型提取音色、语速、响度等），并与消费数据（view-rate）进行关联分析，同时考察体裁（genre）和书名（title）作为协变量的交互效应。进一步使用专有的精细参与度指标进行验证。

Result: 即使消费数据有限，声学信息本身与有声书吸引力之间仍存在稳健的关联，且该关联在控制书名效应后依然显著。此结果在使用更精细的专有参与度指标后得到进一步验证。

Conclusion: Narration 的声学特征对有声书吸引力具有可量化的独立影响，不受书名等内容的完全约束，揭示了利用数据驱动手段优化有声书个性化推荐和 narrator 选角的潜力。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [180] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VRRL强化学习框架，通过轨迹前缀掩码和经验回放缓冲区两个组件，显式训练LVLMs在视觉推理中进行有视觉依据的自我反思，显著提升了分布外场景下的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在进行思维链推理时，虽然具备自我反思能力，但在反思过程中往往未能充分关注视觉输入，导致无法将反馈转化为有视觉依据的修正，尤其在分布外图像上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VRRL（视觉反思强化学习）框架，包含两个核心组件：（1）训练时随机掩码轨迹前缀，迫使模型从错误的中间预测中恢复，而非仅仅避免早期犯错；（2）引入基于经验回放缓冲区的缓冲roll-in，使模型暴露于多样化的失败状态，从而学习纠正这些错误。

Result: 在包含表格和图表的视觉定位任务以及空间导航基准上，VRRL在分布外场景下的平均准确率显著优于标准强化学习基线方法和面向反思的微调基线方法，而现成模型和传统微调模型在分布偏移下性能大幅下降。

Conclusion: 通过VRRL框架显式训练视觉依据的自我反思能力，可以有效提升LVLMs在分布外场景下的鲁棒性和准确性，验证了强化学习在培养模型反思与纠错能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [181] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模说话人识别基准数据集DramaSR-532K（含53.2万条标注对话、900+角色），并构建基于大型推理模型的DramaSR-LRM方法，通过多模态工具调用自主聚合上下文证据，在短话语场景下显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇电视剧的剧情理解高度依赖说话人识别（将每句台词准确归因到对应角色），而现有方法在短话语场景下因声学生物特征不可靠而表现不佳，亟需融合听觉、语言和视觉线索的综合解决方案。

Method: （1）构建DramaSR-532K大规模基准数据集，包含53.2万条标注对话行、900+独特角色，要求融合听觉、语言和视觉多模态线索；（2）提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大型推理模型（LRM），通过多模态工具调用的方式自主聚合上下文证据，综合多种输入实现高保真的说话人归因。

Result: 实验表明DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在短话语（声学生物特征本身不可靠）场景下优势突出。

Conclusion: DramaSR-532K填补了大规模多模态说话人识别基准的空白，DramaSR-LRM通过推理模型与工具调用机制有效整合多模态上下文信息，为长视频理解中的角色归因任务提供了强有力的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [182] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LACUNA测试平台，通过在预定义参数中注入合成PII来评估遗忘方法的参数级定位精度。发现现有SOTA方法虽输出表现良好，但定位不精确且易受重浮现攻击；而精确定位后，即使简单梯度方法也能实现鲁棒遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准仅评估输出层面，无法判断知识是否真正从参数中抹除还是仅被混淆。重浮现攻击的成功加剧了这一担忧，需要参数级真值定位的测试平台来弥合差距。

Method: 构建LACUNA测试平台：通过掩码持续预训练，将合成个体的PII注入1B和7B OLMo模型的预定义参数中，实现参数级真值定位。以此基准测试SOTA遗忘方法的定位精度、擦除效果及抗重浮现攻击鲁棒性。

Result: 现有SOTA方法尽管输出层面表现强劲，但定位精度差且易受重浮现攻击；而当定位成功时，简单的基于梯度的方法即可实现强擦除和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确的参数定位是遗忘成功的关键。LACUNA填补了从行为评估到参数级评估的空白，推动鲁棒、基于定位的遗忘研究。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Continuous-Time Bayesian Networks with Structured Shrinkage Priors for Modelling Multimorbidity Trajectories in Large-Scale Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01549)
*Oyebayo R. Olaniran,Soumya S. Paria,Mizanur Khondoker,Alexander J. MacGregor,Alexandra Lewin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化贝叶斯连续时间贝叶斯网络（CTBN）框架，用于从纵向电子健康记录中学习疾病依赖网络。该框架引入阶次依赖收缩先验以控制高阶交互参数的组合爆炸，并通过模拟研究比较四种稀疏诱导先验，最终在UK Biobank数据中识别出以糖尿病为核心的心血管代谢簇和联系呼吸与特应性疾病的炎症簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重长期疾病（MLTCs）通过疾病间复杂的时间依赖交互发展而来，但现有方法难以同时对疾病进展、共病网络和高维风险因素进行联合建模。尤其在高阶交互参数呈组合增长的情况下，如何在保持主效应可解释性的同时有效控制模型复杂度，是当前方法面临的核心挑战。

Method: 提出结构化贝叶斯连续时间贝叶斯网络（CTBN），允许疾病转移强度依赖于现有疾病状态、成对疾病交互及外生协变量。为控制高阶交互参数的组合爆炸，引入阶次依赖收缩先验——对高阶效应施加递增惩罚，同时保留临床可解释的主效应。通过广泛模拟研究系统比较四种稀疏诱导先验：spike-and-slab、结构化正态先验、贝叶斯LASSO和正则化horseshoe。

Result: 模拟研究中，spike-and-slab先验在网络恢复、变量选择准确性和假发现控制方面均表现最优，而连续收缩先验在硬变量选择任务中效果较差。在UK Biobank的33,558名参与者数据上，spike-and-slab模型识别出两个主导疾病模块：（1）以糖尿病为核心的心血管代谢簇；（2）联系呼吸系统疾病与特应性疾病的炎症簇。

Conclusion: 结构化贝叶斯CTBN框架能有效从纵向电子健康记录中学习有向疾病依赖网络，spike-and-slab先验在稀疏性和变量选择方面具有显著优势。该方法揭示了不同疾病模块间通过中介疾病形成的关联路径，为理解多重长期疾病的发生发展机制提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Multiple long-term conditions (MLTCs) arise through complex, time-dependent interactions among diseases, yet existing methods often struggle to jointly model disease progression, multimorbidity networks, and high-dimensional risk factors. We propose a structured Bayesian continuous-time Bayesian network (CTBN) framework for learning directed disease-dependency networks from longitudinal electronic health records. The model allows disease transition intensities to depend on existing conditions, pairwise disease interactions, and exogenous covariates. To control the combinatorial growth of interaction parameters, we introduce order-dependent shrinkage priors that increasingly penalise higher-order effects while preserving clinically interpretable main effects. We compare four sparsity-inducing priors, spike-and-slab, structured normal, Bayesian LASSO, and regularised horseshoe through extensive simulation studies. Across multiple data-generating scenarios, the spike-and-slab prior achieved the best network recovery, variable-selection accuracy, and false-discovery control, while continuous shrinkage priors were less effective for hard variable selection. The proposed framework was applied to UK Biobank primary care records, focusing on data from 33,558 participants who were free of the ten selected most prevalent conditions at age 40 and who subsequently developed at least one of these conditions during the follow-up period. The selected spike-and-slab model identified two dominant disease modules: a cardiometabolic cluster centred on diabetes and an inflammatory cluster linking respiratory and atopic conditions.

</details>


### [184] [Lancaster copulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01558)
*Angelo Efoevi Koudou,Yves I. Ngounou Bakam,Denys Pommeret*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的Copula——Lancaster Copula，它基于连续Lancaster概率的正交展开构建。作者推导了该Copula及其密度的无穷级数表示，研究了截断效应，并通过数值实验表明低阶截断即可提供精确近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Copula族在建模复杂的非线性相依结构时存在局限性，而基于正交展开的Lancaster概率体系为构建灵活且可解释的相依模型提供了新途径。作者旨在将Lancaster概率理论引入Copula领域，从而拓展相依建模的工具箱。

Method: 1. 利用连续Lancaster概率的正交展开理论，构造Lancaster Copula及其密度函数的无穷级数表示；2. 对无穷级数进行有限阶截断，并理论分析截断效应（包括截断误差边界和收敛性）；3. 通过数值实验验证低阶截断在实际近似中的精度表现。

Result: 1. 成功推导出Lancaster Copula及其密度的无穷级数展开形式；2. 截断分析表明该级数具有良好的收敛性质；3. 数值实验证实，即使采用低阶截断，也能获得高精度的Copula近似，验证了该方法在实际应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: Lancaster Copula是一类具有坚实理论基础的新型Copula族，其基于正交展开的构造方式兼具灵活性与计算可行性。低阶截断即可保证精度，这使得该方法在金融风险管理、统计建模等高维相依结构建模场景中具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce a new copula class, called Lancaster copulas, built from orthogonal expansions of continuous Lancaster probabilities. We derive infinite-series representations for the copula and its density, study truncation effects, and show in numerical experiments that low-order truncations already provide accurate approximation.

</details>


### [185] [From Subgroups to Population Composition: A Transportability Approach to Effect Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01717)
*Michael Cheung,Candus Shi,Kara E Rudolph,Valérie Garès,Caroline A Thompson,Tarik Benmarhnia*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的异质性分析概念框架：通过将群体层面的暴露效应建模为效应修饰因子分布的函数，利用可迁移性方法估计不同效应修饰因子流行率下的效应，进而识别异质性的关键驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的异质性分析方法依赖参数回归和预设亚组，无法捕捉复杂的效应修饰模式；而现代数据自适应方法虽能预测高维异质效应，但增加了方法学复杂性且难以揭示异质性的关键驱动因素。因此需要一种既能灵活建模又不失解释性的新方法。

Method: 提出三步法框架：(1) 基于先验知识选择混杂因素和效应修饰因子（或使用数据自适应方法学习）；(2) 利用可迁移性（transportability）方法估计具有不同效应修饰因子流行率的假设群体中的暴露效应；(3) 将估计效应建模为流行率值的函数。输出包括：效应修饰因子流行率增加所致的群体水平效应变化量，以及跨多种修饰因子和流行率的效应排序。

Result: 利用人口与健康调查（DHS）数据，展示了该方法在干旱对儿童发育迟缓异质性效应中的应用，并开发了Shiny应用以便于在任何场景中实施该方法。

Conclusion: 该概念性方法通过建模群体层面效应曲面与效应修饰因子分布的关系，为异质性分析提供了兼具灵活性和可解释性的新工具，特别适用于可迁移性应用、成本效益分析和干预优先级排序。

Abstract: Identifying heterogeneous populations across which exposure effects vary is essential for transportability applications, cost-benefit analyses, and intervention prioritization. Traditional methods for heterogeneity analyses rely on parametric regression with prespecified subgroups, which may fail to capture complex patterns of effect modification. While recent data-adaptive methods improve high-dimensional heterogeneous effect prediction, they add methodological complexity to analyses and may offer limited insight into key drivers of heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose a novel, conceptual approach for heterogeneity analyses that considers how exposure effects would differ in populations with different compositions by modeling the population-level effect surface as a function of the distribution of effect modifiers. The approach consists of three steps: i) selecting confounders and effect modifiers based on prior knowledge (or alternatively using data-adaptive methods to learn effect modifiers), ii) estimating exposure effects in hypothetical populations with different effect modifier prevalences using transportability methods, and iii) modeling the estimated effects as a function of prevalence values. This approach provides two types of outputs: estimation of the change in the population-level exposure effects attributable to increases in effect modifier prevalence and ranking of effect estimates across multiple effect modifiers and prevalences to identify population characteristics most strongly associated with differential vulnerability. We demonstrate the approach using Demographic and Health Surveys data to examine heterogeneous effects of drought on child stunting and provide a Shiny application to implement this approach in any setting.

</details>


### [186] [Pattern-Calibrated Multimodal Prediction under Blockwise Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01821)
*Junhan Yu,Kejian Zhang,Doudou Zhou,Guojun Zhu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MOSAIC 是一个面向多模态数据中分块缺失的模式校准框架，通过学习共享与模态特定表示、利用与目标模式重叠的可用表示进行初步预测，再基于目标模式数据估计校准间隙，从而在不同缺失模式间实现信息借用而不混合各模式的预测规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将多模态分块缺失视为不完全输入问题，采用插补、零填充或单一池化预测器虽能跨模式借用信息，但会混合不同模式的预测规则。作者关注的是针对预设观测模态模式的预测问题，需要在跨模式借用信息的同时，保持各模式预测规则的独立性。

Method: 提出 MOSAIC 框架：首先学习共享表示与模态特定表示；然后利用与目标模式重叠的可用表示拟合第一阶段预测器；最后基于目标模式数据估计校准间隙（calibration gap）进行校正。理论分析建立了非渐近误差界，将误差分解为重叠有效样本量、校准间隙和表示学习误差三部分。

Result: 非渐近界阐明了跨模式借用何时优于局部拟合，以及这种改善何时受到规则不匹配或表示学习误差的控制。模拟实验验证了表示恢复和目标模式校正效果；在 ICU 死亡率预测、情绪识别和青光眼分类三个应用中，当目标模式样本有限或模式特定规则存在差异时，MOSAIC 均表现出性能提升。

Conclusion: MOSAIC 提供了一种在分块缺失场景下既实现跨模式信息借用又保持模式特异性预测规则的有效方案，理论分析和实验结果共同支持了该方法在目标样本稀缺和模式间规则异构场景中的优势。

Abstract: Blockwise missingness in multimodal data is usually treated as an incomplete-input problem. We instead focus on prediction for a prespecified observed-modality pattern, where the observed modality set determines the information on which the prediction rule can condition. A procedure that imputes missing modalities, zero-fills unobserved modalities, or trains a single pooled predictor may borrow information across patterns, but it can also mix pattern-specific prediction rules. We propose Multimodal Overlap-aware Shared-specific Alignment and Inter-pattern Calibration (MOSAIC), a pattern-calibrated framework for borrowing across missingness patterns without collapsing their prediction rules. MOSAIC learns shared and modality-specific representations, uses the available representations that overlap with the target pattern to fit a first-stage predictor, and then estimates the calibration gap from target-pattern data. We establish non-asymptotic bounds that decompose the error into overlap effective sample size, calibration gap, and representation-learning error, clarifying when cross-pattern borrowing improves over local fitting and when the improvement is controlled by rule mismatch or representation-learning error. Simulations examine representation recovery and target-pattern correction, and applications to ICU mortality prediction, emotion recognition, and glaucoma classification show gains when target-pattern samples are limited or pattern-specific rules differ.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Laplace: Closed-form wrapped Gaussian posterior approximations on statistical manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01909)
*Marcelo Hartmann,Luu Hoang Phuc Hau,Anton Mallasto,Albert Kjøller Jacobsen,Georgios Arvanitidis,Søren Hauberg,Håvard Rue,Mark Girolami*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对贝叶斯统计中Laplace近似局限于椭圆形状的问题，利用对比函数（contrast functions）理论，在赋予Fisher-Rao度量和先验分布几何的统计流形上，推导出指数映射和对数映射的可计算近似，从而绕开了现有wrapped Gaussian方法中求解测地线方程、Jacobi场及计算Christoffel符号和曲率张量等高计算成本步骤，在保持捕获复杂后验几何能力的同时实现了数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laplace近似虽计算高效但仅能刻画椭圆形状；基于黎曼几何的wrapped Gaussian方法虽可捕获偏态、厚尾等复杂后验特征，但采样需通过指数映射求解测地线方程，密度评估依赖对数映射和Jacobi场，涉及昂贵的微分方程求解器及逆矩阵、Christoffel符号、曲率张量等几何量计算。亟需一种既保留几何灵活性又大幅降低计算开销的方法。

Method: 采用对比函数（contrast functions）理论，在赋予Fisher-Rao度量和先验分布几何的统计流形上，构建指数映射和对数映射的易处理近似。该方法完全绕开了对几何量（逆矩阵、Christoffel符号、曲率张量）和数值求解器（微分方程求解）的需求，从而消除了现有wrapped Gaussian方法的主要计算瓶颈。

Result: 在多个模型上的实证结果表明，所提出的近似方法能够捕获复杂的后验几何结构（如偏态、厚尾、狭窄高概率区域），同时在计算速度上比当前最先进的近似方法快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过对比函数理论导出的易处理近似，成功在保持wrapped Gaussian方法几何表达能力的同时，大幅降低了计算成本，为贝叶斯推断中复杂后验的高效近似提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: In Bayesian statistics, the Laplace approximation provides a computationally efficient approximation to posterior distributions. However, its Gaussian form restricts it to elliptical shapes, limiting its ability to capture important posterior features such as skewness, heavy tails, and narrow high-probability regions. Recent work has addressed this limitation by exploiting Riemannian geometry to push forward Gaussian distributions from the tangent space to the manifold, referred to wrapped Gaussians. While offering greater flexibility, they introduce substantial computational challenges. Sampling requires solving geodesic equations through the exponential map and density evaluation additionally depends on the logarithmic map and Jacobi fields, involving costly differential equation solvers and geometric quantities such as inverse matrices, Christoffel symbols and curvature tensors. To overcome these limitations, we employ the theory of contrast functions to derive tractable approximations of the logarithmic and exponential maps on statistical manifolds endowed with the Fisher--Rao metric and the prior distribution geometry. The resulting methodology bypass the need to compute these geometric quantities and numerical solvers thereby removing the principal computational bottlenecks of existing wrapped Gaussian approaches. Empirical results across a range of models demonstrate that the proposed approximation captures complex posterior geometries while remaining orders of magnitude faster than current state-of-the-art approximation.

</details>


### [188] [Moment-Based Selection of Multiresponse Linear Mixed-Effects Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01971)
*Yifan Chen,Yuedong Wang,Guo Yu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MOMENT 提出了一种基于矩方法的逐步框架，利用二阶交叉矩恒等式对混合效应模型中的随机效应协方差矩阵和固定效应系数进行同步选择与估计，通过正半定约束下的对角稀疏性将问题转化为光滑约束凸优化，并用投影梯度下降高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多变量纵向数据中，需要对随机效应协方差结构和固定效应进行联合选择与估计，但现有方法难以在保证正半定性、计算效率和理论保证的同时实现两者的统一处理。

Method: 提出 MOMENT 框架：(1) 利用二阶交叉矩恒等式建立矩条件；(2) 在正半定约束下对随机效应协方差矩阵的对角线施加稀疏性，将选择问题转化为光滑约束凸优化；(3) 使用投影梯度下降算法求解；(4) 在联合 sub-Weibull 误差下建立有限样本理论保证。

Result: 模拟研究表明 MOMENT 整体表现具有竞争力，且当响应变量存在相关性时显著优于单独的单变量分析；在血液透析数据集的应用中，该方法为多变量纵向数据提供了可解释且灵活的建模方案。

Conclusion: MOMENT 是一种有效的混合效应模型选择与估计方法，兼具计算效率、理论保证和实际可解释性，适用于多变量纵向数据的分析。

Abstract: We propose MOMENT (\textbf{MO}ment-Based \textbf{M}ixed-\textbf{E}ffects Selectio\textbf{N} and Es\textbf{T}imation), a stage-wise moment-based framework that exploits second-order cross-moment identities to select and estimate the random-effects covariance matrix and fixed-effects coefficients. By inducing sparsity through its diagonal under a positive semidefinite constraint, the random-effects selection problem reduces to a smooth constrained convex optimization problem that can be solved efficiently by projected gradient descent. We further establish finite-sample theoretical guarantees for the proposed procedure, including random-effects selection consistency and fixed-effects selection consistency under joint sub-Weibull errors. Simulation studies show that MOMENT performs competitively overall and can substantially outperform separate univariate analyses when responses are correlated. An application to the hemodialysis dataset demonstrates that the proposed method yields an interpretable and flexible approach for multivariate longitudinal data.

</details>


### [189] [Plausibility: Exact inference in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01976)
*Stefan Böhringer,Jesse Swen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍了R包plausibility，该包实现了Plausibility理论框架，可用于广义回归模型的精确推断，包括对glmnet等惩罚回归模型的检验，并支持通过类机制进行扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Plausibility理论框架虽然能够在一般参数族中进行精确推断，但缺乏便捷的软件实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供用户友好的R包以推广该框架在实际数据分析中的应用。

Method: 基于Plausibility理论框架，开发了面向回归模型的R包plausibility；采用基于类的面向对象机制，使包易于扩展；支持与glmnet等惩罚回归模型的兼容检验；通过多个R内置数据集进行功能演示。

Result: 成功实现了plausibility R包，覆盖了广泛的回归模型类别，并展示了其在真实数据集上的应用效果；包的计算实现及其对实际数据分析的影响也得到了阐述和讨论。

Conclusion: plausibility包为回归模型的精确推断提供了实用工具，填补了Plausibility框架在软件实现上的空白，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Plausbility is a theoretical framework that allows to conduct exact inference in general parametric families. We introduce R-packages {\em plausibility} that implements this framework for a wide class of regression models. Plausibility can also be used to test penalized regression models such as estimated by package {\em glmnet}. We illustrate the package using a number of R data sets Through a class-based mechanism, the package can be easily extended. We illustrate and discuss computation aspects of the implementation and their impact on real-data analysis.

</details>


### [190] [Grouped Orthogonal Arrays from Orthogonal Arrays and Difference Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02027)
*Meixin Liu,Chunyan Wang,Guangzhou Chen,C. Devon Lin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了多种分组正交阵列的新构造方法，显著扩展了现有分组正交阵列的目录，能够实现更多分组数量和更大分组规模的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着计算机实验和物理实验中分组输入场景的日益增长，需要灵活且高效的设计方法。在这些场景中，不同组别因子之间的交互作用被认为可忽略不计，而已有的分组正交阵列在分组数量和分组规模上均十分有限，无法满足实际需求。

Method: 本文开发了若干新的构造方法，用于生成分组正交阵列，这些构造方法能够系统地产生具有更多分组和更大分组规模的新型设计。

Result: 所提出的构造方法成功生成了一大批新的分组正交阵列，这些阵列在分组数量和每组规模上均显著超越了现有目录中的设计。

Conclusion: 该研究有效扩展了分组正交阵列的设计空间，为计算机实验和物理实验中具有分组输入结构的场景提供了更丰富、更灵活的设计选择。

Abstract: Grouped orthogonal arrays were introduced to address experimental design problems arising in computer experiments with grouped inputs, as well as in physical experiments where interactions between factors from different groups are assumed to be negligible. Motivated by the growing need for flexible and efficient designs under such settings, this article develops several constructions to expand the existing catalogs of grouped orthogonal arrays. The proposed constructions provide a large collection of new grouped orthogonal arrays with significantly larger numbers of groups and group sizes.

</details>


### [191] [Sequential Structure-Sensitive Residual Diagnostics for PDE Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02101)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于e-processes的结构敏感序贯诊断方法，用于检测反问题中传统残差范数检验无法发现的结构化模型误差。该方法通过一组空间残差模式专家组合，以随时有效的方式控制第一类错误，并在三个反问题中验证了其有效性：相比传统方法，e-process能更早地检测到模型误设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平滑反问题中，结构化模型误差可能在观测空间中被衰减，导致残差范数低于预设阈值但残差仍存在相干模式。这使得传统的残差范数诊断（如Morozov偏差准则）会接受存在偏差的模型，导致参数估计、预测或感兴趣的量出现实质性错误。因此需要一种对残差结构敏感的、可随时进行的诊断方法。

Method: 提出基于e-processes的序贯诊断框架：（1）构建一组空间残差模式专家组合（portfolio），每个专家对应字典中的一种残差模式；（2）随观测逐步处理，以似然比形式更新各专家的财富（wealth）；（3）当聚合财富超过预设阈值时拒绝拟合模型；（4）该方法对固定拟合模型提供随时有效的第一类错误控制（anytime-valid type-I error control）；（5）拒绝后，专家财富可归因于特定残差模式，为探索性模型修正提供依据。

Result: 在三个反问题上进行验证——椭圆扩散方程、二维Stokes流、以及基于社区有限元模型icepack的冰川冰流反演。结果表明：标准偏差检验接受了产生实质性错误的误设模型；结构敏感的批量检验能利用完整数据集检测到这些失败；而e-process方法仅需部分观测即可更早地检测到问题。

Conclusion: 相比传统的Morozov偏差检验、固定样本残差检验和批量投影检验，所提出的e-process序贯诊断方法具有三项优势：能检测残差范数检验遗漏的结构化误差；能以随时有效的方式控制第一类错误；能从部分观测中提前发现模型误设，并利用专家财富归因残差模式以指导模型修正。

Abstract: Computational models in science and engineering are often assessed by checking whether the residual norm is consistent with the assumed noise level. This can be misleading in smoothing inverse problems: structured model errors may be attenuated in observation space, leaving residual magnitudes below practitioner discrepancy thresholds while coherent residual patterns remain. As a result, residual-norm diagnostics can accept fitted models that still give biased parameters, predictions, or quantities of interest. We propose a structure-sensitive sequential diagnostic based on e-processes. The method uses a portfolio of spatial residual-pattern experts, updates their likelihood-ratio wealth as observations are processed, and rejects the fitted model when the aggregate wealth crosses a prescribed threshold, giving anytime-valid type-I error control for a fixed fitted model. We compare the method with Morozov discrepancy checks, fixed-sample residual tests, and batch projection tests. Across three inverse problems (elliptic diffusion, two-dimensional Stokes flow, and a glaciological ice-stream inversion implemented in the community finite-element model icepack) we demonstrate how standard discrepancy checks accept misspecified fits that produce materially wrong quantities of interest. Structure-sensitive batch tests detect these failures using the full dataset, while the e-process detects them earlier from a fraction of the observations. After rejection, the expert wealth attributes the evidence to residual patterns in the chosen dictionary and provides a basis for exploratory model correction.

</details>


### [192] [Conformal Bayes for Two-Sided Censored Gaussian Regression under Label Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02173)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对标签偏移下响应变量存在双侧截尾（censored）的预测问题，提出了一种混合空间下的共形贝叶斯方法。通过结合后验预测倾斜（posterior predictive tilting）与加权共形校准，该方法可在截尾高斯模型（Tobit模型）下生成包含边界原子和内部区间的混合最高密度区域预测集，并通过潜在指数倾斜导出观测空间中的混合校准权重（两个原子比率加一个内部密度比率），从而恢复边缘覆盖并缩小预测集规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标签偏移场景下，当响应变量被双侧截尾时（低于L或高于U的值被记录为边界值），观测到的预测分布变为混合分布（在L和U处有概率原子，在(L,U)上有连续密度），传统的共形预测方法无法直接适用。核心困难在于：潜在标签偏移无法在截尾观测尺度上直接给出普通的密度比率，需要一种能同时处理原子和连续成分的校准与预测集构造方法。

Method: 作者提出"加权倾斜共形贝叶斯"（weighted tilted conformal Bayes）方法：(1) 使用双侧Tobit高斯贝叶斯预测头，结合拉普拉斯后验近似；(2) 通过后验预测倾斜得到包含左原子、内部区间、右原子三部分构成的倾斜预测分布，并推导出三项闭合形式的归一化常数；(3) 潜在指数倾斜在截尾边界处诱导出尾部平均的原子权重，而在内部保持密度比率，从而构建混合观测空间校准权重（两个原子比率+一个内部密度比率）；(4) 权重修正校准测度，预测倾斜则给出目标自适应的混合HDR几何结构。

Result: 合成实验表明：与加权源分数校准（weighted source-score calibration）相比，加权倾斜共形贝叶斯能恢复边缘覆盖，且预测集更小；同时揭示了边缘覆盖与原子/内部观测的组件级行为之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了截尾响应下标签偏移的共形预测问题，混合HDR预测集可灵活适应不同程度的截尾（强截尾时可退化为纯原子集），为截尾数据下的不确定性量化提供了新工具。

Abstract: Prediction under label shift becomes nonstandard when responses are censored. In a two-sided censored Gaussian model, latent values below $L$ and above $U$ are recorded at the boundary values, so the observed predictive distribution is mixed, with atoms at $L$ and $U$ and a continuous density on $(L,U)$. In this paper we develop conformal Bayes for this mixed-space setting by combining posterior predictive tilting with weighted conformal calibration. Under a two-sided Tobit Gaussian Bayesian prediction head with a Laplace posterior approximation, the tilted predictive distribution has left-atom, interior, and right-atom components, with a three-term closed-form normalizer. The resulting prediction set is a mixed highest density region that can combine boundary atoms with an interior interval and can reduce to atom-only sets under strong censoring. The main technical issue is that latent label shift does not directly give an ordinary density ratio on the observed censored scale. A latent exponential tilt induces tail-averaged atom weights at the censored boundaries, while the interior ratio remains density based. This yields a mixed observed-space calibration weight with two atom ratios and one interior density ratio. The weight corrects the calibration measure, while predictive tilting gives target-adapted mixed-HDR geometry. Synthetic experiments show that weighted tilted conformal Bayes restores marginal coverage with smaller sets than weighted source-score calibration, while revealing a trade-off between marginal coverage and component-wise behavior across atoms and interior observations.

</details>


### [193] [Goodness of Fit Tests Based on Joint Densities of Multiple Sample Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02285)
*Roman Guchenko*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出基于模拟置信集的拟合优度检验方法，包含两类：一是利用超矩形置信集对主成分次序统计量进行检验；二是基于k近邻方法构建最高密度区域的任意形状置信集。同时探讨了双样本推广及概率积分变换以外的变换方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟合优度检验方法在统计功效和适用范围上存在局限。作者旨在开发基于联合分布置信集的新型检验方法，以提升对多种备择假设的检验功效，并将方法拓展至更高维统计量向量和双样本场景。

Method: 第一类方法：对次序统计量及相关统计量向量的主成分构建超矩形置信集进行检验，并与经典检验、Zhang检验及图形检验比较。第二类方法：采用k近邻方法检测最高密度区域，构建任意形状的置信集，应用于次序统计量、经验分布函数值、矩以及经典拟合优度统计量的组合。双样本扩展通过置换检验实现，并比较矩版本与能量距离置换检验。此外，探讨了以标准正态等作为目标分布的变换方法。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在功效上具有竞争力，且通常优于现有方法。在正态零分布下，第一主成分对应样本均值，第二主成分与方差的线性模拟相关。基于k近邻的检验方法对多种备择假设表现出高效力。

Conclusion: 基于模拟置信集的拟合优度检验方法功效优越，适用范围广。k近邻方法比传统核密度方法更适合高维场景。将数据变换至其他目标分布（如标准正态）可进一步提升检验效能，是一种有前景的策略。

Abstract: We propose goodness-of-fit tests based on simulated confidence sets for joint distributions of multiple sample statistics, focusing on absolutely continuous null distributions with known parameters. One class of tests uses hyperrectangular confidence sets for principal components of order statistics and related statistic vectors. Extending earlier work on horizontal and vertical confidence bands for cumulative distribution functions, these tests are compared with some classical, Zhang, and related graphical tests. Simulations show that the proposed procedures are competitive with, and often more powerful than, existing methods. We also study the geometry of principal-component-based statistics; under a normal null distribution, the first principal component corresponds to the sample mean, while the second is related to a linear analogue of variance.
  A second class of tests uses confidence sets of arbitrary shape constructed through highest density regions. Unlike earlier kernel-density-based approaches, we use a k-nearest-neighbor method for detecting highest density regions, which is better suited to higher-dimensional statistic vectors. We study tests based on order statistics, empirical distribution function values, moments, and combinations of classical goodness-of-fit statistics. The resulting procedures are powerful against a wide range of alternatives.
  We also outline a two-sample extension via permutation tests based on joint distributions of several statistics and compare moment-based versions with energy-distance permutation tests. Finally, we discuss transformations other than the probability integral transform, showing that mapping data to another target distribution, such as the standard normal, can be advantageous when powerful tests are available for that distribution.

</details>


### [194] [MATCH: Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses in Non-Euclidean Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02295)
*Leheng Cai,Xu Guo,Qirui Hu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出 MATCH 方法，利用样本分割与独立随机乘子在 held-out 损失上构造非退化高斯主导项，为 Fréchet 回归提供统一的显著性检验与模型设定检验框架，涵盖全局显著性、部分显著性及模型充分性检验，并通过交叉拟合与 p 值合并提升稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Fréchet 回归中，检验非欧数据是否符合目标模型面临一个核心困难：原假设下 oracle 损失一致，导致普通的 held-out 损失差是一阶退化的，即插件统计量被冗余估计误差主导。需要一种无需残差或切空间坐标即可构造非退化检验统计量的通用方法。

Method: 提出 MATCH（Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses）框架：(1) 采用样本分割，将数据划分为估计样本与 held-out 样本；(2) 在 held-out 损失上施加独立随机乘子，构造非退化高斯主导项，避免显式计算残差或切空间坐标；(3) 进一步引入交叉拟合检验与重复交叉拟合配合 p 值合并，提升数据利用效率与稳定性。框架统一处理全局显著性、部分显著性和全局 Fréchet 回归充分性三种检验场景。

Result: 理论证明：建立原假设下渐近有效性、固定备择假设下的一致性以及局部功效保证。模拟实验在分布型响应、对称正定矩阵响应和球面响应三种场景下验证了理论结果。实证应用包括县级家庭收入分布检验和北大西洋热带气旋位置数据分析。

Conclusion: MATCH 为 Fréchet 回归中条件假设检验提供了一个统一、灵活且理论严密的框架，成功规避了一阶退化问题，不依赖切空间坐标或残差估计，在理论和实践中均表现出良好性能。

Abstract: We propose a new procedure MATCH (Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses) to test whether the non-Euclidean data match the target model, which is a general framework for significance and specification testing in Fréchet regression. MATCH covers global significance, partial significance, and the adequacy of global Fréchet regression, providing a unified way to compare unrestricted conditional Fréchet means with restricted alternatives. One of the key challenges is that the ordinary held-out loss difference is first-order degenerate under the null: the oracle losses coincide, and plug-in statistics is dominated by nuisance estimation error. MATCH uses sample splitting and independent random multipliers on held-out losses to create a nondegenerate Gaussian leading term without residuals or tangent-space coordinates. To improve data use and stability, we further develop cross-fitted tests and repeated cross-fitting with p-value merging. We establish asymptotic null validity, consistency under fixed alternatives, and local power guarantees. Simulations for distributional, symmetric positive-definite (SPD) matrix-valued, and spherical responses support the theoretical findings, and applications to county-level household income distributions and North Atlantic tropical-cyclone locations demonstrate the practical use of the proposed tests.

</details>


### [195] [Cross-Audit Projection for Model Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02328)
*Yijian Huang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对二分类问题中的模型风险预测，论文发现K折交叉验证在估计类别特定风险时表现可能劣于经验估计量。作者提出了一种称为交叉审计投影(CAP)的两步法，结合重抽样估计过乐观偏差和渐近理论投影进行偏差校正，实现了一阶等价于经验风险、二阶渐近无偏的优良性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K折交叉验证被广泛用于缓解经验风险估计的过乐观问题，但本文通过数值研究发现，在基于经验风险最小化的二分类中，K折CV在估计类别特定风险时表现不佳，甚至比经验估计量更差。这一反直觉现象亟需理论解释和更好的方法来解决。

Method: 作者首先通过高阶渐近分析揭示了K折CV收敛速度较慢的原因，以及经验估计量存在二阶渐近偏差导致过乐观的机制。在此基础上提出CAP两步法：(1)交叉审计步骤：采用与K折CV相同的重抽样方案在子样本中估计过乐观程度；(2)投影步骤：利用渐近理论信息，对经验风险进行样本量缩减后的偏差校正。同时开发了配套的推断程序。

Result: CAP估计量在理论上具有一阶渐近等价于经验风险、二阶渐近无偏的优良性质。模拟研究证实了CAP的理论优势并展示了良好的有限样本表现。在乳腺癌检测的实际应用中进一步验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: K折交叉验证在类别特定风险估计中未必可靠，CAP方法通过交叉审计与渐近投影的结合，有效克服了这一局限，为模型风险预测提供了更准确、理论上有保证的替代方案。

Abstract: For training-data-based model risk prediction, $K$-fold cross-validation~(CV) is widely used to mitigate the well-known over-optimism of the empirical risk and is often regarded as reliable. However, for binary classification via empirical risk minimization, our numerical studies reveal a surprising phenomenon: $K$-fold CV may perform poorly in estimating class-specific risks, even worse than the empirical estimator. We perform a higher-order asymptotic analysis showing that $K$-fold CV may converge at a slower rate, whereas the empirical estimator exhibits a second-order asymptotic bias that explains its over-optimism. These findings motivate a novel two-step procedure for model risk prediction, termed cross-audit projection (CAP). The cross-audit step adopts the same resampling scheme as $K$-fold CV to estimate over-optimism in subsamples, while the asymptotic-theory-informed projection step adjusts for the reduced sample size in bias correction of the empirical risk. The resulting CAP estimator is first-order asymptotically equivalent to the empirical risk while achieving second-order asymptotic unbiasedness. An accompanying inference procedure is also developed. Simulation studies support theoretical advantages of CAP and demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance. An application to breast cancer detection further illustrates the proposed method.

</details>


### [196] [Merging of Bayes and quasi-Bayes empirical Bayes procedures for Poisson compound decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02340)
*Stefano Favaro,Sandra Fortini*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究泊松复合决策问题，比较了基于狄利克雷过程后验的贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略与基于牛顿算法的准贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略。作者为非参数估计的混合分布建立了浓度速率，并将其转化为遗憾值的衰减速率，证明了两种策略的频率学派合并结果。数值实验表明准贝叶斯策略在保持相当精度的同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松复合决策问题是统计学中的一个经典问题，传统上使用经验贝叶斯方法在混合模型下估计泊松均值。基于牛顿算法的准贝叶斯策略（g-modeling）计算效率高，但其与基于狄利克雷过程的贝叶斯策略之间的理论关系尚未得到充分论证，亟需理论上的桥接与证明。

Method: 在泊松混合模型框架下，假设存在一个真实的oracle混合分布：1) 推导由贝叶斯和准贝叶斯估计诱导的边缘概率质量函数的浓度速率；2) 将浓度速率转化为对应遗憾值（即超额贝叶斯风险）的衰减速率；3) 利用上述速率证明两种经验贝叶斯策略之间的频率学派合并（merging）结果；4) 将分析从一维扩展至多维泊松复合决策问题；5) 通过合成数据数值实验对比两种策略的精度与计算资源消耗。

Result: 理论层面：建立了贝叶斯和准贝叶斯两种非参数g-modeling策略的边缘分布浓度速率与遗憾衰减速率，并据此证明了二者的频率学派合并性质。数值层面：准贝叶斯策略在达到与贝叶斯策略可比估计精度的同时，所需计算资源显著减少，在多维场景下优势尤为突出。

Conclusion: 准贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略不仅在计算效率上具有显著优势，而且在理论上与基于狄利克雷过程的贝叶斯策略具有渐近等价性，这为准贝叶斯方法在泊松复合决策问题中的广泛应用提供了坚实的理论支撑。

Abstract: The Poisson compound decision problem is a long-standing problem in statistics, in which empirical Bayes methods are used to estimate Poisson means under a mixture model. We study this problem from the viewpoint of $g$-modeling, comparing two nonparametric strategies for estimating the unknown mixing distribution: a Bayesian empirical Bayes strategy, based on the Dirichlet process posterior, and a quasi-Bayesian empirical Bayes strategy, based on Newton's algorithm. The latter is computationally attractive, but its relationship with the Bayesian strategy requires theoretical justification. Under a Poisson mixture model with a ``true'', or oracle, mixing distribution, we establish concentration rates for the marginal probability mass functions induced by the Bayesian and quasi-Bayesian estimates. These rates are then translated into rates of decay for the corresponding regrets, interpreted as excess Bayes risks, and used to prove a frequentist merging result between the Bayesian and quasi-Bayesian empirical Bayes strategies. We also extend the analysis to the multidimensional Poisson compound decision problem. Numerical experiments on synthetic data illustrate that the quasi-Bayesian strategy achieves accuracy comparable to the Bayesian strategy, while requiring substantially fewer computational resources, especially in the multidimensional setting.

</details>


### [197] [Inference for Group Interaction Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02385)
*Jiawei Fu,Cyrus Samii,Ye Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为"群体交互实验"（group interaction experiments）开发了基于设计的推断框架，涵盖固定/随机分组以及是否存在干扰（interference）的多种场景。作者在稀疏抽样渐近体系下，证明了集群稳健推断在存在干扰时仍能提供有效的边缘化暴露效应推断，并引入了一种新颖的耦合策略（coupling strategy）来推导常用估计量的渐近分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实验设计涉及个体被随机分配到不同群体，群体再接受不同水平的处理条件。这种"群体交互实验"在社会科学、教育、公共卫生等领域广泛存在，但现有文献缺乏统一的设计推断框架，尤其当存在个体间干扰（spillover/interference）时，如何进行有效的统计推断尚不明确。

Method: 作者采用基于设计的推断（design-based inference）方法，在稀疏抽样渐近（sparse-sampling asymptotics）框架下，分别对固定群体与随机形成群体、存在干扰与无干扰四种场景进行建模。通过提出一种新颖的耦合策略（coupling strategy），推导了常用估计量的渐近分布，并证明集群稳健标准误（cluster-robust standard errors）在不同干扰来源下的一致性。

Result: （1）存在干扰时，集群稳健推断保持一致，能够有效捕捉多种来源的依赖性，对边缘化暴露效应（marginalized exposure effects）提供有效推断；（2）无干扰且随机分组时，设计退化为个体随机化实验，个体层面的异方差稳健推断即可满足平均处理效应的推断需求；（3）所提出的耦合策略为其他复杂实验中的设计推断提供了通用工具。

Conclusion: 该研究为群体交互实验提供了一个统一的设计推断框架，澄清了不同场景下的因果目标（causal estimand）与适配的推断策略，并通过耦合策略为复杂实验的渐近分析开辟了新路径。

Abstract: A common experimental research design is one in which individuals are randomly allocated into groups that then interact under different group-level treatment conditions. We develop design-based inference for such "group interaction" experiments, covering scenarios in which groups are either fixed or randomly formed and in which potential outcomes are either fixed relative to others' group assignments or subject to interference. For each scenario, we characterize the causal estimand that the design targets and the inferential strategy appropriate to it. Working in a sparse-sampling asymptotic regime, we show that cluster-robust inference remains consistent and accounts for dependencies from various sources when interference is present, delivering valid inference on marginalized exposure effects. When interference is absent and groups are formed randomly, the design reduces to an individually randomized experiment, and individual-level heteroskedasticity-robust inference suffices for the average treatment effect. Our results on the asymptotic distribution of commonly used estimators rely on a novel coupling strategy that may be useful for design-based inference in other complex experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出M-QCDNet，一种将Q矩阵结构先验嵌入神经网络的多层认知诊断模型，结合认知诊断模型的可解释性与深度学习的灵活性，实现可解释、公平且可操作的AI认知诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型具有结构可解释性但灵活性不足，而深度神经网络灵活但缺乏心理测量学的透明度。两者之间存在鸿沟，亟需一种既能保持诊断效度、又具备神经灵活性的混合方法。

Method: 提出的M-QCDNet将Q矩阵作为结构先验融入多层神经网络架构中，约束题目-技能关系，确保潜在掌握概要保持可解释性。同时设计带有L2惩罚项的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不一致的技能激活进行惩罚，平衡预测性能与结构对齐。此外，开发了基于可解释对齐的评估指标，量化预测技能激活与题目级技能的匹配程度。

Result: M-QCDNet在保证预测性能的同时，实现了较高的结构对齐度。通过可解释对齐指标验证了模型能够有效捕捉题目-技能关系，并在课堂实践中支持学习困难的早期发现与基于掌握的干预。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet成功将诊断效度嵌入模型设计，弥合了心理测量透明度与神经网络灵活性之间的鸿沟，为认知诊断领域提供了一种可解释、公平且可操作的人工智能方法，具有重要的理论与实践价值。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [199] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向EEG压力检测的Intra-Inter Riemannian流形注意力网络（IRMAN），通过在频域上独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，结合频率簇聚合和切片内-切片间注意力机制，有效融合频率特异性判别线索与全局时序上下文，在三个数据集上以轻量参数量取得了优于五种SOTA方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG压力检测面临两大核心挑战：一是压力相关的判别模式既具有被试依赖性又具有频率特异性，传统黎曼方法仅在时域建模空间协方差，忽视了关键的神经振荡信息；二是标准的时间token化方式割裂了EEG切片间的时序连贯性，导致上下文信息丢失。

Method: 提出IRMAN网络，包含三个核心设计：(1) 在每个频率点上独立构建空间协方差矩阵，并映射到SPD切空间，同时保留通道几何结构与频率特异性判别信息；(2) 引入频率簇聚合机制，自动选择具有信息量的频谱成分，形成与EEG节律对齐的紧凑数据驱动频率簇以降低冗余；(3) 设计切片内-切片间注意力模块，自适应整合局部切片级频谱动态与全局时序上下文。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，IRMAN一致超越五种最先进的基线方法，平衡准确率最高达到82.78%，同时仅需1.60M参数量和31.95M FLOPs的计算开销，展现出优异的效率-性能平衡。

Conclusion: IRMAN通过在频域黎曼流形上建模并结合注意力机制融合局部与全局时序信息，有效解决了跨被试EEG压力检测中频率特异性与时序连贯性的双重挑战，为轻量高效的脑电解码提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [200] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究PBE系统的对抗鲁棒性，提出固定集最坏情况污染的形式化框架，发现低边际任务存在随机噪声评估遗漏的对抗脆弱性，并引入版本空间分区聚合防御，但其有效性依赖于清洁语义保持分区投票边际。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE鲁棒性研究主要建模随机噪声（如打字错误），忽略了自适应攻击者针对合成器选择最具破坏性示例的对抗威胁。作者旨在揭示这一被忽视的失败模式，并探索可能的防御机制。

Method: (1) 形式化固定集最坏情况污染框架，针对有限PBE版本空间；(2) 实现在有限池内的精确搜索和启发式污染搜索，针对字符串变换DSL；(3) 提出版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御：将示例划分为不相交组分别合成，再通过语义签名投票决策。

Result: 在策划/生成的DSL任务上，单次编辑即可翻转全部8个spike任务，而200次随机打字错误、DSL池和距离匹配随机对照的成功率仅为10.3%、11.0%和16.7%；生成的边际为1的行在预算1下被翻转，但VPA可恢复；在公开SyGuS PBE_SLIA切片上，投票边际接近1，自适应攻击可将VPA准确率降至零；Playgol在141个接受行上对打字错误和同池随机对照显示正向配对bootstrap差距。LLM范围检查显示局部和API模型在受控边际为1任务上呈现相同从清洁到攻击的定性模式。

Conclusion: 低边际PBE任务存在显著的对抗鲁棒性维度，随机噪声评估无法捕捉。VPA作为防御仅在清洁语义保持分区投票边际时有效，在现实任务中常失效。该研究划定了一个有意的边界，部分结果偏负面，揭示了当前PBE系统在对抗环境下的根本局限。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [201] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于心电图识别，通过将PRQST关键标志点作为领域知识，构建双流有向图分别建模心电周期内和周期间的时空关系，在中文心电图智能竞赛数据集上取得了优于现有模型的性能，尤其在罕见类别上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型在医疗等专业领域（如心电图识别）中的可解释性不足，且现有端到端卷积神经网络未充分利用心电图的领域先验知识（如PRQST关键标志点），导致模型性能受限，尤其对罕见类别检测效果不佳。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络：1）引入PRQST关键标志点作为领域知识；2）构建双流有向图——空间有向图捕获关键点间的位置关系，时间有向图刻画相邻心动周期间的时序依赖；3）在图结构上进行卷积操作实现心电信号分类（九分类）。

Result: 在首届中文心电图智能竞赛数据集上，整体平均F1分数达88.1%，罕见类别平均F1分数达76.3%，均超越现有最先进模型，验证了领域知识的引入有效提升了检测性能，特别是对罕见类别的提升显著。

Conclusion: 融合领域知识（PRQST关键点）的图卷积网络能有效建模心电信号的时空结构，不仅提升了整体分类精度，更显著改善了罕见类别的识别能力，为可解释的心电图AI诊断提供了新思路。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [202] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多探针网格（multiprobe grid）近似最近邻搜索算法进行了系统性的$N$和$d$维度扩展分析，揭示了其在GloVe嵌入数据集上存在此前未报道的维度扩展交叉点——网格方法保持近似恒定的维度扩展指数，而其他方法吞吐量下降，表明其在重索引或高维场景中具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前近似最近邻搜索的扩展性分析中，基于网格的方法长期被忽视。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究多探针网格算法在数据集规模和维度上的扩展行为，并将其与现代ANN方法进行对比。此外，由于自注意力机制可形式化为ANN操作，ANN算法的扩展特性亦可为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。

Method: 采用多探针网格（multiprobe grid）算法，在GloVe嵌入家族数据集上进行系统的扩展性实验。考察维度$d$和数据集规模$N$两个维度的扩展行为，并与基于图（graph-based）、基于树（tree-based）以及基于划分（partitioning-based）的ANN方法进行对比，重点关注吞吐量、维度扩展指数、查询扩展性和索引构建成本等指标。

Result: 实验发现了一个此前未报道的$d$扩展交叉现象：在GloVe嵌入数据集上，多探针网格的维度扩展指数近似恒定，而其他图、树和划分类方法的吞吐量随维度增加而显著退化。同时，多探针网格在$N$上呈现近线性查询扩展，且索引构建成本低于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 基于网格的方法（如多探针网格）在重索引频繁或高维场景中具有竞争力，其优势来源于维度鲁棒性和低索引成本。该工作不仅复兴了网格方法在ANN中的地位，也为高效Transformer架构的设计提供了来自ANN扩展性质的参考视角。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [203] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文概述了一本名为《从逼近到涌现》的专著，该书以统一的、证明导向的框架梳理了现代深度学习理论，从经典的逼近、优化和泛化基础，延伸到过参数化、鲁棒性、生成模型、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放法则、可解释性、对齐及涌现等前沿机制，旨在为研究者提供一幅严谨而完整的深度学习理论全景图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已发展到超越任何单一数学解释的复杂程度，现有文献中理论成果分散孤立，缺乏一条将这些理论串联起来的统一叙事脉络。该书的动机在于将这些碎片化的理论结果整合为一个连贯的研究图景，揭示各理论所控制的对象、所依赖的假设条件以及尚未解释的现象，从而为学界提供一幅系统化、结构化的深度学习理论地图。

Method: 该书采用"统一框架+证明导向"的组织方式。每一理论均围绕三个核心维度展开分析：(1) 理论所控制的核心对象是什么；(2) 使该理论成立的关键假设是什么；(3) 该理论未能解释的现象有哪些。全书按逻辑递进路径编排，从经典基础（逼近论、优化论、泛化论）出发，逐步过渡到现代机制（过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放法则、可解释性、对齐、涌现）。

Result: 该专著整合出一条从经典理论到前沿机制的完整研究叙事，揭示出现代深度学习理论的核心命题正日益聚焦于：习得的机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练过程中涌现出来。同时也坦诚地指出，现有理论虽强大却仍不完整。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论目前处于一种"强大但不完整"的状态，且研究重心正从传统的孤立分析转向一个更根本的问题——规模、数据、架构与训练如何共同催生习得机制的涌现。该书为数学功底扎实的研究者和从业者提供了一份严谨的当下理论指南。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [204] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 IonSense-QKG 框架，为公共锂电池数据集标注量子计算就绪元数据（任务类型、模态、编码、量子比特范围等），并引入量子就绪评分（QRS）以筛选适合混合量子-经典机器学习工作流的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共锂电池数据集在化学、模态、规模、标签质量等方面差异巨大，直接影响其是否适用于近期混合量子-经典机器学习工作流，但缺乏一个系统性的量子就绪度评估与数据集发现机制。

Method: 基于 EV-Battery-IonSense 索引，为公共电池数据集记录丰富量子相关元数据（任务类型、感知模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围、NISQ 可行性），并设计透明的量子就绪评分（QRS）作为数据集选择启发式方法；提供元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接校验工具及 SQL 风格查询示例。

Result: 框架实现了基于查询的元数据发现，可识别适合紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG 将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为以数据为中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [205] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了条件推断树（CIT）和条件推断森林（CIF）作为top-k特征排序方法的表现，通过真实数据基准测试、运行时消融实验和合成特征恢复实验，证明CIF在下游预测任务中具有较强的竞争力，并分析了自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIT和CIF通过在选择分裂阈值前检验特征来减少分裂选择偏差，但重复的排列检验和阈值搜索使其计算开销较大。现有研究对其作为top-k特征排序方法在真实数据集上的系统评估不足，且缺乏对其运行时瓶颈的深入理解。

Method: 作者采用三类实验设计：（1）在22个分类数据集和8个回归数据集上比较CIF与其他方法的预测性能排名；（2）通过运行时消融实验，分析自适应停止、阈值搜索数量等因素对拟合时间和下游分数的影响；（3）通过稀疏高维合成模拟，考察森林特征采样对信息特征遗漏的影响。理论上，证明了在给定节点下，若特征和排列预算不依赖于节点响应，Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛排列p值可在完全排列零假设下控制节点级别的拒绝率。

Result: CIF在17种分类方法中排名第4（22个数据集），在18种回归方法中排名第3（8个数据集）。消融实验显示：关闭自适应停止使拟合时间增加4.0–8.4倍，使用精确阈值搜索增加1.9–10.8倍，而下游分数变化最多仅0.011。稀疏高维模拟表明，森林特征采样可能导致许多分裂决策中遗漏信息特征。

Conclusion: CIF在所评估的下游预测基准中是一种有效的top-k特征排序方法。自适应停止和阈值搜索数量是影响运行时的最大因素，且其调整对预测性能影响很小，这为实际应用中权衡精度与效率提供了指导。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [206] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归的甲基化CNS肿瘤分类方法，在91类分类任务中达到86%准确率（比现有最优方法高4个百分点），在甲基化类别家族级别达到93%（高5个百分点），具有临床显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNA甲基化CNS肿瘤分类方法在跨队列可迁移性、方法学严谨性和多类别鲁棒评估方面仍存在挑战，需要一种方法论上更严谨的机器学习方法来提升分类可靠性和临床实用性。

Method: 提出一种结合稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）进行降维、多项逻辑回归（Multinomial Logistic Regression）进行分类的机器学习方法。在2,801例参考队列上使用分层3折交叉验证，在1,104例独立临床验证队列上进行外部评估，分别在91类精细级别和甲基化类别家族级别两个粒度上评估性能。

Result: 在2,801例参考队列上，分层3折交叉验证平均准确率达96%。在1,104例独立验证队列上，91类级别准确率为86%，甲基化类别家族级别准确率为93%，相比现有最优方法的82%和88%分别提升了约4个和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 所提方法在方法论上更加严谨，在各项评估设置中一致优于现有最优方法，5个百分点的提升在临床上可直接影响肿瘤亚型判定、治疗选择及下游临床决策，能实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [207] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"三项缩放定律"，将模型大小、训练数据量显式分解为训练步数和批量大小，能够正确恢复最优批量大小的缩放行为，且可利用次优批量大小的训练运行进行鲁棒拟合，所需训练运行量显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缩放定律通常将训练数据量视为整体，未能显式区分训练步数和批量大小对模型性能的影响。这导致无法直接刻画最优批量大小的缩放规律，且拟合缩放定律需要大量、昂贵的训练运行。作者希望提出一个更精细的缩放定律，能够显式建模批量大小的影响，从而降低拟合成本并更好地理解最优批量大小的缩放行为。

Method: 作者提出了一种"三项缩放定律"（three-term scaling law），将模型性能的损失函数表达为模型参数量（N）、训练步数（S）和批量大小（B）三个独立变量的函数。通过在大量训练运行上拟合该定律，验证其正确恢复最优批量大小缩放行为的能力。此外，该方法能够利用次优批量大小的训练数据，因此只需更少的训练运行即可实现鲁棒拟合。作者还从该定律出发，推导了次优批量大小下的缩放规律。

Result: 所提出的三项定律能够正确恢复最优批量大小的缩放行为；由于可利用次优批量大小的训练运行，该定律在显著减少训练运行数量的情况下仍可鲁棒拟合；从该定律推导出的次优批量大小缩放规律与先前关于临界批量大小（critical batch size）的经验发现一致。

Conclusion: 三项缩放定律通过显式分离训练步数和批量大小，提供了比传统缩放定律更精细的建模能力，既能降低拟合成本，又能统一解释最优与次优批量大小下的缩放行为，并与先前关于临界批量大小的经验研究吻合。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [208] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私和分布式学习泛化误差之间的关系并非简单的三元权衡，而是取决于隐私强度的非单调关系：强隐私下增加隐私反而降低泛化误差，弱隐私下则相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作建立了拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私(LDP)与优化误差之间的三元困境，但该困境是否推广到泛化误差尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过推导LDP约束下拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习的算法稳定性的匹配上下界，理论分析泛化误差在不同隐私强度下的行为，并辅以实证评估验证。

Result: 理论证明：(1)高噪声/强隐私机制下，增加隐私会降低泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私间无张力；(2)低噪声/弱隐私机制下，鲁棒性与隐私间张力重现，增加隐私会损害泛化性能。实证结果佐证了这一非单调行为。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性、LDP与泛化误差之间的关系并非普遍的三元困境，而是关键取决于隐私机制所处区间，呈现非单调特征。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [209] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 即使用户只能通过黑盒API获取每个解码token的单个logit（无logit bias功能），NightVision攻击仍能通过"公共集提示"技术和频谱分析，结合TTFT测量，估算出LLM的隐藏维度、深度和参数量等架构参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前商业LLM提供商为保护模型架构机密，已将其API限制为仅暴露每个token的单个logit，并移除了logit bias功能。本文旨在探究在这种最严格的API限制下，是否仍存在泄露LLM架构参数（隐藏维度、深度、参数量）的可能性。

Method: 提出NightVision攻击方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 公共集提示（Common Set Prompting）技术：通过设计多个提示，使它们在输出位置上共享同一组token的log概率，然后对这些log概率矩阵进行频谱分析（奇异值分解），从奇异值分布中推断前馈网络的隐藏维度；(2) 利用端到端首token时间（TTFT）测量，结合已估算的隐藏维度，进一步推断模型的深度和总参数量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上实证评估：隐藏维度恢复的平均相对误差约为23%（MoE模型上仅9%）；对于超过30亿参数的模型，深度和参数量的恢复误差约53%。消融实验展示了估算精度如何随token预算和模型属性变化。

Conclusion: 当前商业LLM API的限制措施（仅暴露单个logit、禁用logit bias）仍不足以完全隐藏底层模型的架构细节，NightVision证明了即使在最严格的黑盒条件下，关键架构参数仍可被有效恢复。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [210] [Role-Aware Neural Convex Divergence Heads for Asymmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01762)
*He Huang,Lu Shen,Yunfeng Huang,Li Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种角色感知的神经凸散度头（role-aware neural convex divergence head），用于非对称表示学习，通过在输入凸神经Bregman散度之前引入源角色和目标角色投影，在角色投影空间中产生非负结构化评分，实验表明该方法在多个有向关系基准上一致优于普通ICNN-Bregman头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多表示学习问题涉及有向关系（如词汇蕴含、句子蕴含、本体层级、引用链接），但标准欧氏距离、余弦距离和马氏距离都是对称的，无法建模方向性；而通用神经评分器虽能建模方向性但缺乏几何结构。因此需要一种既能捕捉方向性、又具有良好几何解释性的结构化距离模块。

Method: 提出角色感知神经凸散度头：首先对输入表示应用源角色和目标角色投影，然后在角色投影空间中计算输入凸神经网络（ICNN）参数化的Bregman散度，得到非负结构化评分。论文对该方法进行了理论分析，包括投影空间恒等性、源角色凸性、方向间隙分解以及基于Hessian矩阵的局部曲率刻画。

Result: 在词汇、句子、本体和有向图基准上的实验表明：（1）角色感知投影在主要语义和本体基准上（十次随机种子）一致提升方向性准确率，且保持零负散度率；（2）在大型固定特征引用预测任务上，专门的对称或双曲基线在排序准确率上仍更强。整体上，该方法适合作为有向关系任务中的结构化、可解释即插即用距离模块。

Conclusion: 角色感知神经凸散度头是一种结构化且可解释的即插即用距离模块，适用于有向关系重要的任务。角色投影能稳定提升普通ICNN-Bregman头的方向性准确率，但在大规模固定特征场景下，专用对称/双曲基线仍有优势。

Abstract: Many representation learning problems involve directed relations, such as lexical entailment, sentence entailment, ontology hierarchy, and citation links. Standard Euclidean, cosine, and Mahalanobis heads are symmetric, while generic neural scorers can model directionality but provide limited geometric structure. This paper proposes a role-aware neural convex divergence head for asymmetric representation learning.
