A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.

</details>


### [281] [Predicting Early Stages Of Alzheimer's Disease And Identifying Key Biomarkers Using Deep Artificial Neural Network And Ensemble Of Machine Learning Methodologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02142)
*Debopriya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于堆叠集成学习的机器学习模型，用于阿尔茨海默病早期检测。该模型在ADNI数据集上，通过迭代插补、Borderline SVM-SMOTE处理缺失值和类别不平衡，结合包装法与嵌入式特征选择，最终以逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN和LightGBM为基分类器构建堆叠模型，并与人工神经网络对比，旨在寻找最佳分类器并识别关键生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期症状隐匿、易与正常衰老混淆，导致诊断延迟；目前尚无根治手段，但早期检测有助于及时干预和病情管理。因此，亟需一种能利用临床、神经心理学及神经影像等多模态数据实现早期精准检测的机器学习方法。

Method: 采用ADNI数据集，依次进行：(1)迭代插补处理缺失值；(2)Borderline SVM-SMOTE解决类别不平衡；(3)包装法与嵌入式方法进行特征选择；(4)特征缩放后，构建以逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN和LightGBM为基学习器的堆叠集成模型，同时训练人工神经网络作为对比，最终以精确率、召回率、F1分数和AUC-ROC评估模型性能。

Result: 该摘要未报告具体实验结果（如各项指标的数值），仅说明了模型比较的评估框架和预期目标——找出最佳分类器并识别有助于AD早期诊断的重要生物标志物。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统化的数据预处理与集成学习策略，构建了一个面向阿尔茨海默病早期检测的机器学习框架，其潜在贡献在于同时追求模型性能优化与生物标志物发现，为临床辅助诊断提供了可行思路。

Abstract: Alzheimers disease (AD) is a brain disorder that develops slowly and mainly affects memory, thinking, language, and daily activities. It is one of the most common causes of dementia and creates many difficulties for patients as well as their families. In the early stage, the symptoms are often mild and may look like normal ageing. For this reason, many people are diagnosed late, when the disease has already progressed. At present, there is no complete cure for AD. Still, early detection can help doctors manage the condition better and take suitable steps at the right time. In this study, a machine learning model is proposed to detect the early stages of Alzheimers disease using clinical details, neuropsychological test scores, and neuroimaging-related measures. The data used in this work is collected from the Alzheimers Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI). As the dataset has missing values, iterative imputation is applied to fill them. The dataset also has class imbalance, which is handled using Borderline SVM-SMOTE. After that, feature selection is carried out using wrapper-based and embedded methods so that only important features are used for training. The selected features are divided into training and testing sets, and feature scaling is applied. A stacking ensemble model is developed using Logistic Regression, Extra Trees, Bagging KNN, and LightGBM as base classifiers. Along with this, an artificial neural network is also trained on the same dataset. The performance of these models is compared using precision, recall, F1-score, and AUC-ROC. This study aims to find the best classifier and also identify important biomarkers that may help in the early diagnosis of Alzheimers disease.

</details>


### [282] [Dynamic Neural Graph Encoding of Inference Processes in Deep Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02166)
*Di Wu,Huan Liu,Zhixiang Chi,Yuanhao Yu,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于动态图的神经网络参数表示方法（DNG-Encoder），保留推理的时序特性，并构建INR2JLS用于隐式神经表示分类，在CIFAR-100-INR上超越SOTA约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理神经网络权重空间的方法通常忽略网络推理中层间处理的顺序性，且高维权重空间的高效处理仍具挑战。本文旨在利用推理的时序动态特性来改进权重空间的表示学习。

Method: 提出动态图表示神经网络参数，捕获推理过程中的时序动态；设计DNG-Encoder（动态神经图编码器）处理这些图，保留神经处理的顺序性；进一步构建INR2JLS（隐式神经表示到联合潜在空间）框架，将隐式神经表示映射到联合潜在空间以支持下游任务。

Result: 在多项任务上取得显著提升，尤其在CIFAR-100-INR数据集上的INR分类准确率超越现有最先进方法约10%。

Conclusion: 通过动态图建模神经网络参数的时序推理特性是一种有效策略，DNG-Encoder和INR2JLS框架显著提升了INR分类等下游任务的性能。

Abstract: The rapid advancements in using neural networks as implicit data representations have attracted significant interest in developing machine learning methods that analyze and process the weight spaces of other neural networks. However, efficiently handling these highdimensional weight spaces remains challenging. Existing methods often overlook the sequential nature of layer-by-layer processing in neural network inference. In this work, we propose a novel approach using dynamic graphs to represent neural network parameters, capturing the temporal dynamics of inference. Our Dynamic Neural Graph Encoder (DNG-Encoder) processes these graphs, preserving the sequential nature of neural processing. Additionally, we also leverage DNG-Encoder to develop INR2JLS (Implicit Neural Representation to Joint Latent Space) for facilitate downstream applications, such as classifying Implicit Neural Representations (INRs). Our approach demonstrates significant improvements across multiple tasks, surpassing the state-of-the-art INR classification accuracy by approximately 10% on the CIFAR-100-INR.

</details>


### [283] [Privacy-Preserving and Verifiable Approximate Distributed Coded Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02187)
*Xavier Martínez-Luaña,Alba Gude-Santos,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关的对抗鲁棒分布式学习框架，联合解决联邦学习和去中心化学习中的隐私保护与恶意行为防御问题。框架将特定范式的防御机制与通用隐私增强编码计算技术GPBACC相结合，并通过攻击驱动的实证分析验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式学习防御方案通常孤立地处理隐私泄露和恶意操纵两类威胁，且往往局限于特定学习范式（联邦学习或去中心化学习）或模型架构，缺乏统一的部署框架。现实场景亟需一个同时覆盖隐私保护和对抗鲁棒性的通用解决方案。

Method: 提出模型无关的对抗鲁棒分布式学习框架。核心包括：（1）GPBACC——一种适用于任意模型的隐私增强编码计算技术；（2）联邦学习场景下集成鲁棒聚合策略以抑制恶意参与者影响；（3）去中心化学习场景下采用近似解码-比较与群组测试技术实现轻量级验证和敌手隔离，无需可信聚合器。

Result: 通过攻击驱动的实证评估，实现了代表性的隐私攻击和恶意行为。实验表明，GPBACC与鲁棒聚合及验证机制相结合，能够显著降低隐私泄露并提升对主动敌手的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 隐私增强编码计算与适当的对抗防御策略相结合，为安全分布式机器学习提供了实用且可部署的基础。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning enables collaborative model training without centralizing data, but it also exposes learning processes to privacy leakage and malicious manipulation. Existing defenses typically address these threats in isolation and are often tailored to specific learning paradigms or model architectures, limiting their applicability in realistic deployments. In particular, federated learning and decentralized learning exhibit distinct adversarial surfaces that are rarely addressed within a unified framework. In this paper, we present a model-agnostic framework for adversary-resistant distributed learning that jointly addresses privacy preservation and malicious behavior across both federated and decentralized settings. Our approach combines paradigm-specific defense mechanisms with GPBACC, a privacy-enhancing coded computing technique applicable to arbitrary machine learning models. For federated learning, we integrate robust aggregation strategies to mitigate the impact of malicious participants, while for decentralized learning we employ approximate decode-and-compare and group testing techniques to enable lightweight verification and adversary isolation without relying on a trusted aggregator. Crucially, we evaluate the proposed framework through an explicit, attack-driven analysis. We implement representative privacy attacks and malicious behaviors, and empirically demonstrate that the combination of GPBACC with robust aggregation and verification mechanisms significantly reduces privacy leakage and improves resilience against active adversaries. These results suggest that privacy-enhancing coded computing, when combined with appropriate adversary-resistance strategies, provides a practical and deployable foundation for secure distributed machine learning.

</details>


### [284] [An Optimisation Framework for the Well-Conditioned Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02194)
*Joseph Webb,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSGNAR是一个基于双重草图高斯-牛顿法的可扩展二阶优化框架，结合自适应正则化与步长控制策略，大幅提升了物理信息神经网络(PINNs)求解偏微分方程的精度与速度，在多个基准问题上取得了前所未有的低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在求解偏微分方程时，由于损失景观严重病态(ill-conditioned)，优化过程极其困难，导致其精度始终无法媲美经典数值求解器。本文旨在通过设计更强大的二阶优化方法，从根本上解决这一病态条件问题。

Method: 提出DSGNAR框架，核心包括：(1)双重草图高斯-牛顿模型(doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton)，通过随机草图技术降低二阶优化中Hessian矩阵的计算与存储开销；(2)自适应比例策略，精细控制正则化强度与步长。该方法具有良好的可扩展性，并对网络架构、算术精度和初始超参数具有鲁棒性。

Result: 在非线性、混沌、多尺度、高维及Navier-Stokes等问题的测试中，DSGNAR实现了双精度下低至3×10⁻¹⁶的相对ℓ₂误差；在Burgers方程上比现有结果提升五个数量级；在高维Poisson问题上提升八个数量级；单精度下可在十秒内求解Burgers方程至4.75×10⁻⁷的相对误差。

Conclusion: DSGNAR通过二阶优化有效克服了PINNs的病态损失景观，在精度和速度上均大幅超越现有方法，使PINNs首次达到可与经典求解器媲美的精度水平。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising route to solve partial differential equations, yet they have struggled to reach the precision of classical solvers. The obstacle is increasingly understood to be one of optimisation, owing to the severely ill-conditioned loss landscape. We present $\textbf{DSGNAR}$: Doubly-Sketched Gauss-Newton with Adaptive Ratio, a scalable second-order optimisation framework that confronts this ill-conditioning and, in doing so, obtains unprecedented accuracy and speed. $\textbf{DSGNAR}$ couples a doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton model with a novel strategy that carefully controls both regularisation and step length. Across a suite of problems spanning nonlinear, chaotic, multi-scale, high-dimensional, and Navier-Stokes, the framework greatly improves on the state of the art: able to attain relative $\ell_2$ errors as low as $3\times10^{-16}$ in double precision, improve contemporary results by five orders of magnitude on the canonical Burgers' equation, and as much as eight orders on a high-dimensional Poisson problem, while remaining markedly faster. We further show that, in single precision, solutions at the limit of round-off error can be obtained very quickly: Burgers' equation to $\ell_2^{\text{rel}} = 4.75 \times 10^{-7}$ in under ten seconds. The framework is also robust to the choice of architecture, arithmetic precision, and initial hyperparameters.
  The code is available at https://www.github.com/wephy/physics-informed-neural-networks

</details>


### [285] [Online Resource Allocation with Continuous Random Consumption: Regret under Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02196)
*Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究连续分布奖励与消耗下的在线资源分配问题，引入"活跃加权质量指数 p"刻画 regret 的阶，提出一个样本路径边际策略，在无流体非退化假设下实现 $O(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})$ 或 $O((\log T)^2)$ 的 regret。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典在线资源分配问题通常假设奖励或资源消耗为离散或特定分布，且依赖流体松弛的非退化性。本文旨在突破这些限制，处理奖励和消耗均为连续分布、且流体松弛可能退化（primal degeneracy / dual non-uniqueness）的更一般设定，并精确刻画 regret 的结构性来源。

Method: 建立连续分布下的在线资源分配模型，定义"活跃加权质量指数 p"来量化价值-消耗比位于接受阈值附近的请求的规模加权质量。当 p > 1 时证明 regret 下界为 $\Omega(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})$，并设计样本路径边际策略（sample-path marginal policy）匹配该下界（至多差 polylog 因子）；p = 1 时 regret 为 $O((\log T)^2)$。分析通过具体分布示例（如规模与价值比独立均匀时 p=1，规模与奖励独立均匀时 p=2）加以说明。

Result: Regret 下界为 $\Omega(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})$（对所有 p > 1 成立）；所提样本路径边际策略匹配该下界（差 polylog 因子），p = 1 时达到 $O((\log T)^2)$。整个正则性类中均实现 $o(\sqrt{T})$ regret，无需流体非退化假设。

Conclusion: 加性 regret 由价值-消耗比接近接受阈值的请求的规模加权质量所支配，通过活跃加权质量指数 p 统一刻画问题难度。所提策略在涵盖 primal degeneracy 和 dual non-uniqueness 的广泛正则性类中均接近最优，显著拓宽了在线资源分配问题的理论适用范围。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation when both rewards and consumption sizes may be continuously distributed. Requests arrive sequentially and must be accepted or rejected irrevocably under fixed resource capacities. Each request belongs to one of finitely many observable types; conditional on an observable request type, both the reward and the scalar size are random, and the realized size scales a fixed type-specific resource-consumption vector. The model allows the deterministic fluid relaxation to be degenerate.
  We show that additive regret is governed by the size-weighted mass of requests whose value-to-size ratios lie near the active acceptance cutoffs. We formalize this quantity through an active weighted-mass exponent p. When p > 1, this cutoff mass is thin, and the problem is genuinely hard: every online policy must incur regret of order at least $T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)}$, and this holds for every p > 1. A sample-path marginal policy matches this lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors; and when p = 1, so that the mass grows linearly near the cutoff, it attains $O((\log T)^2)$ regret. For example, if the size and the value-to-size ratio are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 1; if instead the size and the reward are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 2. Thus the policy achieves $o(\sqrt{T})$ regret throughout this regularity class without any fluid non-degeneracy assumption, allowing both primal degeneracy and dual non-uniqueness.

</details>


### [286] [Self-explainable Operator Learning for Discovering Spatial Patterns in Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02203)
*Mojgan Alishiri,Amirhossein Arzani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自解释的算子学习框架，将算子学习重新表述为广义函数线性模型的线性组合，通过积分方程的可加分解性实现输入域划分与局部贡献评估，从而直接揭示哪些空间特征驱动预测。在血流和空气动力学问题中验证了其有效性，并与事后解释方法定性一致，但具有无需外部工具的内嵌可解释性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的算子学习模型虽然强大，但本质上是不透明的"黑箱"模型，用户无法理解其预测背后的推理过程。现有的可解释性方法多为事后（post-hoc）工具，需要额外的外部分析手段。作者试图将可解释性直接内嵌到算子学习框架本身，使得模型天然具备解释能力。

Method: 核心思想是将算子学习重新表述为基于积分方程表达的广义函数线性模型（GFLM）的线性组合。利用这些积分方程的可加分解性，将输入域划分为若干子域，对每个子域计算局部积分，从而量化每个区域对最终预测的贡献。这种分解使得模型能够将特定输入区域与对应输出模式直接关联，实现输入-输出双向解释。

Result: 在血流问题和非定常空气动力学两类流体流动问题上进行了验证，涵盖函数到标量和函数到函数两种映射类型。结果表明，算子通常优先关注具有强特征梯度的区域，提供了物理上有意义的决策洞察。与已有的post-hoc可解释性方法定性比较显示出一致性，同时突显了本方法的核心优势——可解释性直接嵌入算子结构，无需外部工具。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种数学透明、物理可解释的方法来揭示数据中的关系，通过使数据驱动的物理系统分析更具可解释性，增强了科学应用中机器学习模型的可信度。

Abstract: Operator learning has emerged as a powerful tool for modeling complex physical systems in functional spaces. However, their neural network-based architectures make them opaque models, obscuring the reasoning behind their predictions. In this work, we introduce a self-explainable operator learning framework that overcomes this challenge by reformulating operator learning as a linear combination of generalized functional linear models expressed through integral equations. Exploiting the additive decomposability of these integral equations, we divide the input domain into subdomains and compute localized integrals to evaluate the contribution of each region to the final prediction. This decomposition enables direct interpretability where the model explains both inputs and outputs by linking specific input regions to corresponding output patterns, thereby revealing which spatial features drive predictions. We demonstrate the framework on function-to-scalar and function-to-function mappings in fluid flow problems involving blood flow and unsteady aerodynamics. The results show that the operator most often prioritizes regions with strong feature gradients, providing physically meaningful insight into the model's decision-making process. Comparisons with established post-hoc explainability methods demonstrate qualitative agreement while highlighting the key advantage of the proposed approach: explainability is embedded directly within the operator structure itself and does not require an external tool. Therefore, our framework provides a mathematically transparent and physically interpretable approach to uncover relationships within data, fostering trust in machine learning for scientific applications by enabling more informed data-driven analysis of physical systems.

</details>


### [287] [Generalization in offline RL: The structure is more important than the amount of pessimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02288)
*Max Weltevrede,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Wendelin Böhmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明，离线强化学习中悲观主义是否损害泛化，关键不在于悲观程度，而在于悲观结构是否尊重最优解的对称性。对称的过度悲观反而优于非对称的轻度悲观。基于此，作者提出在策略提取阶段通过一致性损失施加数据增强，而非在训练阶段直接使用增强数据集，并在对称环境中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL中，过度悲观通常被认为会阻碍泛化。本文挑战这一直觉，提出核心问题：悲观主义的**结构**（而非程度）才是决定泛化能力的关键因素，特别是悲观结构是否与CMDP中最优解的对称性对齐。

Method: 首先在上下文MDP框架下进行理论分析，证明对称的过度悲观值函数在泛化上优于非对称的轻度悲观值函数。基于该理论，提出不应按常规做法在增强数据集上进行离线训练，而应在策略提取阶段通过一致性损失（consistency loss）施加数据增强，使悲观结构尊重环境对称性。实验部分结合IQL和CQL算法，在旋转对称的reacher环境中进行验证。

Result: 理论上证明了悲观结构的对称性对泛化至关重要；实验上验证了在策略提取阶段使用一致性损失进行数据增强，优于在增强数据集上直接训练的常规做法。

Conclusion: 离线RL中，成功泛化的关键在于悲观结构是否尊重最优解的对称性，而非悲观的绝对程度。数据增强应通过策略提取时的一致性损失来施加，以更好地对齐对称结构，而非简单地在增强数据集上训练。

Abstract: While pessimism counteracts overestimation bias in offline reinforcement learning (RL), being overly conservative has been associated with hindering certain forms of generalization. However, in this paper we demonstrate that being overly pessimistic does not inherently prevent optimal generalization in contextual MDPs (CMDPs). Instead, we argue successful generalization depends not on the amount of pessimism, but whether the pessimistic structure respects the underlying symmetries of the optimal solution. We prove that a mildly pessimistic, non-symmetric value function can generalize worse than an overly pessimistic, symmetric one. In offline RL, the structure of the pessimism is determined by the structure of the dataset coverage. As such, enforcing a symmetric value function can be non-trivial, and might require techniques such as data augmentation (DA). Inspired by our theoretical results, we argue that DA can best be applied through a consistency loss during policy extraction, rather than the common practice of (regular) offline training on an augmented dataset. This is empirically validated using IQL and CQL on a rotationally symmetric reacher environment.

</details>


### [288] [Optimizing Visual Generative Models via Distribution-wise Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02291)
*Ruihang Li,Mengde Xu,Shuyang Gu,Leigang Qu,Fuli Feng,Han Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布级奖励（distribution-wise rewards）的强化学习微调框架，用于视觉生成模型。通过子集替换策略高效估计分布奖励，并结合后验模型合并系数优化，有效缓解了传统样本级奖励导致的奖励黑客（reward hacking）、模式坍塌和多样性下降问题，在多个基座模型上显著提升了FID指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉生成的强化学习通常使用样本级奖励函数，容易导致奖励黑客问题——即模型为追求高奖励而产生视觉伪影、丧失生成多样性，所有样本独立地向同一方向优化引发模式坍塌。因此，需要一种能考虑样本间数据分布关系的奖励机制来对齐真实数据分布。

Method: 1. 提出分布级奖励（distribution-wise reward），评估一组样本的整体分布质量而非单样本，缓解模式坍塌。2. 引入子集替换策略（subset-replace strategy），仅更新生成参考集的一小部分来高效提供奖励信号，克服分布奖励估计的高计算成本。3. 将强化学习应用于优化后验模型合并系数（post-hoc model merging coefficients），缓解因在常规RL中引入随机微分方程（SDE）导致的训练-推理不一致问题。

Result: 在多个基座模型上取得显著提升：SiT模型的FID-50K从8.30降至5.77，EDM2从3.74降至3.52。定性评估也证实方法在保持样本多样性的同时增强了感知质量。

Conclusion: 基于分布级奖励的RL微调框架能有效对齐真实数据分布，在提升生成质量的同时保持多样性，为解决视觉生成中奖励黑客和模式坍塌问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Conventional reinforcement learning strategies for visual generation typically employ sample-wise reward functions, yet this practice frequently results in reward hacking that degrades image diversity and introduces visual anomalies. To address these limitations, we present a novel framework that finetunes generative models using distribution-wise rewards, ensuring better alignment with real-world data distributions. Unlike rewards that evaluate samples individually, distribution-wise reward accounts for the data distribution of the samples, mitigating the mode collapse problem that occurs when all samples optimize towards the same direction independently. To overcome the prohibitive computational cost of estimating these rewards, we introduce a subset-replace strategy that efficiently provides reward signals by updating only a small subset of a generated reference set. Additionally, we apply RL to optimize post-hoc model merging coefficients, potentially mitigating the train-inference inconsistency caused by introducing stochastic differential equation (SDE) in regular RL practices. Extensive experiments show our approach significantly improves FID-50K across various base models, from 8.30 to 5.77 for SiT and from 3.74 to 3.52 for EDM2. Qualitative evaluation also confirms that our method enhances perceptual quality while preserving sample diversity.

</details>


### [289] [One More Time: Revisiting Neural Quantum States from a Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02292)
*Juan Agustín Duque,Sergio García Heredia,Vinicius Hernandes,Eliška Greplová,Thomas Spriggs,Aaron Courville,Anna Dawid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的神经量子态优化算法——近端波函数优化（PWO），将变分能量最小化视为优势策略梯度问题，通过裁剪振幅通道的概率比和相位通道的相位增量来实现信赖域优化。PWO避免了显式矩阵求逆，可跨多步更新复用样本，兼具一阶优化的可扩展性和理论保证。在伊辛模型和阻挫J₁-J₂自旋系统上，PWO在稳定性和收敛速度上均优于Adam、minSR和SPRING，并成功微调了15亿参数的RWKV-7模型，将NQS优化规模提升了三个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NQS优化方法存在两难困境：Adam可扩展但忽略函数空间几何，随机重配置（SR）原理上有优势但在大模型中计算昂贵且数值不稳定。作者旨在填补这一空白，开发一种既具备SR的理论优势、又具备Adam可扩展性的优化方法。

Method: 将变分能量最小化重新表述为Born分布上的优势策略梯度问题，提出Proximal Wavefunction Optimization（PWO）算法：对振幅通道的概率比变化和相位通道的相位增量分别进行裁剪（clipping），实现信赖域约束。该方法无需显式矩阵求逆，支持多样本跨更新复用。

Result: 在一维和二维伊辛模型及阻挫J₁-J₂自旋系统上，PWO的稳定性和实际收敛时间均优于Adam、minSR和SPRING。此外，在15亿参数的RWKV-7模型上成功进行了微调，展示了远超先前工作的NQS优化规模（提升超过三个数量级）。

Conclusion: PWO成功将信赖域优化引入NQS训练，在保持理论优势的同时实现了与一阶方法相当的可扩展性，为大规模神经量子态的优化开辟了新路径，并通过十亿参数级模型的实验验证了其实际可行性。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) provide a flexible and scalable framework for approximating quantum many-body wavefunctions. Among NQS parameterizations, autoregressive models are especially attractive because they enable exact, independent sampling from the Born distribution, avoiding the autocorrelation and mixing issues of Markov chain methods. Yet their optimization remains comparatively underexplored: Adam is a scalable method but ignores function space geometry, while stochastic reconfiguration is principled but costly and numerically fragile in large models. To address this gap, we show that variational energy minimization can be viewed as an advantage policy-gradient problem over the Born distribution, motivating trust-region optimization for NQS training. We introduce Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO), a principled trust-region algorithm that clips probability-ratio changes in the amplitude channel and phase increments in the phase channel. PWO avoids explicit matrix inversion, reuses samples across multiple updates, and combines the scalability of first-order optimization with theoretical guarantees. Across Ising and frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ one- and two-dimensional spin systems, PWO improves stability and wall-clock convergence over Adam, minSR, and SPRING. Finally, we fine-tune a $1.5$B-parameter RWKV-7 model, demonstrating NQS optimization at a scale over three orders of magnitude beyond prior work.

</details>


### [290] [Self-Gating Attention for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02344)
*Dezheng Wang,Tong Chen,Wei Yuan,Congyan Chen,Shihua Li,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自门控注意力（SGA），一种轻量级即插即用注意力机制，通过共享可学习矩阵捕捉通用注意力模式，辅以输入相关的残差分量，避免标准注意力中Query和Key的投影计算，将时间和内存复杂度从二次降低至线性，在多个真实数据集上实现了与最先进方法相当的预测性能，同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多头自注意力在时间序列预测中存在随回溯长度二次增长的时间和内存复杂度，限制了其在资源受限或高吞吐量系统中的部署。作者通过定性和定量分析发现，时间序列中的自注意力图往往包含跨时间戳的冗余模式——源于真实世界时间序列中重复的时间模式和相对稳定的时间相关性——这为设计更高效的注意力机制提供了契机。

Method: 提出自门控注意力（Self-Gating Attention, SGA）：一种即插即用的注意力机制，将注意力分数表示为共享可学习矩阵与输入相关残差分量的组合。共享矩阵捕捉跨序列的通用注意力模式，残差分量捕捉输入相关的动态变化。SGA摒弃了标准注意力中Query和Key的投影运算，从而将时间复杂度降为线性、分数矩阵内存复杂度降为线性（相对于回溯长度）。SGA被集成到多个预测骨干网络中进行验证。

Result: 在覆盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人类活动和气候记录的9个公开真实数据集上，将SGA与标准自注意力及多种轻量级注意力变体进行对比。结果表明，SGA在公开基准上提升了推理效率，同时保持了与最先进注意力机制相当的预测性能。

Conclusion: SGA作为一种线性复杂度的注意力替代方案，在保持竞争力的预测精度的同时显著降低了推理成本，为时间序列预测模型在实际部署场景中提供了高效的注意力机制选择。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [291] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DecompRL 通过将复杂问题分解为可独立求解的子模块并重新组合，以 CPU 评估替代重复 GPU 采样，在 LiveCodeBench 和 CodeContests 上显著超越标准 RL 基线，尤其解决了传统方法在基础策略概率近乎零时无法求解的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种扩展测试时计算的方法——重复采样和带验证奖励的强化学习——在面对搜索空间过大、基础策略正确概率近乎零的问题时均会失效。前者 GPU 成本随采样次数线性增长，后者牺牲了样本多样性。因此，需要一种能从根本上缩小问题搜索空间的新范式。

Method: 提出 DecompRL，一种显式学习将问题分解为层次化代码结构并独立实现各子模块的强化学习算法。通过将 n 个模块各实现 k 个版本并重新组合，可产生高达 k^n 个候选解，将瓶颈从 GPU 推理转移到廉价 CPU 评估，GPU token 成本降低约 50 倍。

Result: 在 LiveCodeBench 和 CodeContests 基准上，使用 Qwen 2.5 7B 和 Code World Model 32B，DecompRL 在每个问题超过 10^5 tokens 的设定下，性能超越标准 RL 和多样性优化 RL 基线，成功解决了标准生成方法无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 问题分解是突破 LLM 推理能力瓶颈的有效途径。DecompRL 通过模块化生成与重组，以指数级候选解空间和大幅降低的 GPU 成本，证明了"化整为零"策略在代码生成领域的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [292] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuFS 提出利用神经元激活模式替代传统输出级信号进行主动小样本学习样本选择，通过神经元多样性确保覆盖度、通过神经元共识度识别模型易产生幻觉的困难样本，在推理和文本分类任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFSL方法依赖输出级信号（如预测熵、基于外部嵌入的语义相似度）来筛选样本，但忽略了模型内部动态，无法精准定位LLM的特定知识缺口。作者希望直接利用神经元激活模式捕捉模型内部状态，实现更原则性的样本选择。

Method: 提出NeuFS框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 使用神经元激活模式直接表征样本；(2) 双准则选择策略——基于神经元模式多样性保证小样本覆盖广度，基于神经元共识度量化识别LLM易产生幻觉的信息丰富且困难的样本；(3) 在三个数据集上进行推理和文本分类任务的实验评估及消融研究。

Result: 在三个数据集上，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务上均超越现有AFSL基线方法。消融实验进一步表明，内部神经元激活相比外部嵌入提供了更有效、更原则性的选择信号。

Conclusion: 将选择范式从输出级代理信号转向模型内部神经元动态是有效的，NeuFS框架验证了神经元激活模式作为样本选择信号的优越性，为AFSL领域提供了新方向。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [293] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QFedAgent 是一种混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，利用变分量子电路融合模块替换经典多模态融合，在保持97.7%准确率的同时实现约10倍参数缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统产生的异构非独立同分布多模态传感器数据会降低传统联邦学习性能，且经典融合模块引入大量参数开销和通信成本，亟需更高效的融合方法。

Method: 提出 QFedAgent 框架，核心是变分量子电路融合模块：通过量子态编码和量子纠缠建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数（对比经典MLP融合的33K参数），并结合个性化联邦学习策略，在OPPORTUNITY数据集上基于受试者非IID划分进行验证。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上达到97.7%平均测试准确率，总参数量较经典MLP融合方法降低约10倍（72 vs. 33K），证明参数高效的量子融合在联邦学习场景中具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 量子-经典混合融合方案能够在显著降低参数和通信开销的同时，保持与传统联邦基线相当的多智能体活动识别精度，为资源受限环境下的隐私保护联邦学习提供了新路径。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [294] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出极端自适应Transformer（Exformer），通过引入由局部、步长和极端三个稀疏注意力组件构成的极端自适应注意力机制，显式建模正常与极端事件间的时间依赖关系，在四个水文径流数据集上实现了优于现有基线的3天预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer预测模型对所有时间点均匀处理，导致数据中罕见但关键的极端事件（如水文径流峰值）被低估，影响洪水监测与预警系统的准确性。需要一种能同时捕捉正常模式与极端模式依赖关系的预测框架。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，核心是极端自适应注意力机制，由三个稀疏组件构成：（1）Local组件捕获短期时间依赖；（2）Stride组件捕获周期性依赖；（3）Extreme组件选择性建模正常与极端径流模式之间的事件感知依赖。三者协同实现对极端事件的显式建模。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3天预测任务中均优于现有最先进基线模型，验证了显式融入极端感知注意力能有效提升Transformer在不平衡时间序列上的预测能力。

Conclusion: 显式引入极端感知注意力机制可显著增强Transformer模型对含罕见但关键事件的非平衡时间序列的预测能力，为水文预报等领域的极端事件建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [295] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格的理论分析，证明掩码图像建模（MIM）在异构数据下天然比对比学习（CL）更鲁棒，且网络平均连通性越高鲁棒性越强。基于此提出MAR损失，并通过实验验证了理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式自监督学习面临数据异构性的关键挑战，但现有研究对不同D-SSL框架在non-IID设置下的鲁棒性缺乏理论理解，亟需填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者对不同D-SSL框架（MIM与CL）在non-IID数据下的鲁棒性进行严格理论分析，推导出鲁棒性与SSL范式及网络连通性之间的理论关系；基于理论洞见，提出MAR损失——在MIM目标基础上加入局部-全局对齐正则化。

Result: 理论证明：(1) MIM预训练本质上比CL对异构数据更鲁棒；(2) D-SSL鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而提升，因此联邦学习（FL）的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）。实验在多种模型架构和分布式设置下验证了理论洞见及MAR损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来D-SSL算法的设计提供了坚实的理论基础，并展示了理论分析向实用算法（MAR损失）转化的可行性。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [296] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Neuron-OPSD 是一种无需标注的自蒸馏框架，利用内部神经元激活来指导训练数据选择与教师上下文构建，通过 on-policy 蒸馏实现领域内性能提升，同时保持跨领域泛化能力并缓解校准崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无标注自进化方法（如基于 SFT/GRPO 或基于奖励的 on-policy RL）存在两个核心缺陷：一是域外性能退化，二是校准误差膨胀。同时，在专家标注成本高昂的专业领域中，缺乏一种既不需要真实交互反馈也不需要人工监督的后训练方法。

Method: 提出 Neuron-OPSD 框架：1）利用模型内部神经元激活信号来筛选高质量训练数据并构建教师上下文（teacher context）；2）从教师分布进行 on-policy 知识蒸馏训练。整个过程无需任何真实标签，属于数据驱动（data-centric）的自蒸馏范式。

Result: 在专业领域基准测试上，Neuron-OPSD 实现了域内任务性能提升，同时保持了跨领域泛化能力，并有效缓解了此前基线方法中的校准坍塌（calibration collapse）问题。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD 为在线交互或外部监督成本高昂甚至不可行的场景提供了一种有效的后训练方案，且概念上有别于依赖已记录奖励轨迹的离线 RL 方法。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [297] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与传统Adam优化器在训练MLIPs（NequIP、Allegro）上的表现，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，尤其在部分力监督场景下优势突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs领域的研究聚焦于新架构和数据集，而优化器的选择长期被忽视，社区默认使用Adam及其变体，缺乏对更优优化器的系统性探索。

Method: 在NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP模型上，对Adam、Muon、SOAP及SOAP-Muon四类优化器进行系统对比实验，评估收敛速度、最终精度，并特别考察部分力监督条件下的表现差异。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam，鲁棒性强且表现稳定；Muon仅提供部分提升。改进在部分力监督设置下尤为明显。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIPs训练中被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，矩阵结构优化器（尤其是SOAP类方法）有潜力替代Adam成为该领域的新默认选择。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [298] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD 提出一种选择性采纳教师指导的框架，通过构建教师与学生分布的反向KL重心目标，自适应控制逐token的监督强度，从而在保持探索能力的同时有效缓解特权信息泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法中，教师模型基于特权信息（如参考答案）提供密集的token级监督，导致三个关键问题：(1) 对域内模式过拟合，抑制探索；(2) 跨域泛化能力受损；(3) 特权信息泄露——学生习得依赖仅在训练时可用、测试时不可获取的答案相关捷径。因此需要一种既能利用教师指导又不损害学生自主推理能力的蒸馏框架。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心思想是选择性采纳教师指导。具体地：将训练目标设定为教师分布与学生分布的加权几何组合（反向KL重心目标），而非直接拟合完整教师分布；用两分布之间的差异度（discrepancy）自适应控制每个token位置的混合权重；从理论上证明了该方法具备泄露衰减和探索保持两个关键性质。

Result: 在SciKnowEval的四个科学领域上，DemoPSD在性能上超越了GRPO和SDPO；同时保持更高的训练熵，表明探索能力得到保留；在分布外GPQA基准上也展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过反向KL重心目标实现了教师指导与自主探索的有效平衡，从理论和实验两方面验证了其在缓解特权信息泄露、保持探索和提升泛化性方面的优势。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [299] [eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE): Learning Data-Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Priors for Latent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01275)
*Qijun Chen,Shaofan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: X-VAE 用预训练自编码器的经验均值和标准差替代标准正态先验，构建数据自适应高斯先验，并引入隐变量缩放因子以平衡生成多样性与保真度，在保持重构质量的同时提升隐空间对齐度和生成可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 VAE 采用各向同性标准高斯先验，往往无法捕捉复杂数据集的真实隐表示分布，导致重构精度受限、采样质量下降、隐空间表达能力不足。

Method: 提出 X-VAE 框架：(1) 用预训练 AE 隐编码的经验均值和标准差参数化高斯先验，替代标准正态分布；(2) 推导该先验对应的 KL 散度目标函数；(3) 引入隐变量缩放因子，在生成阶段显式控制采样方差，调节多样性与保真度。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上验证，X-VAE 保持重构质量的同时，隐表示与经验数据分布对齐更好，生成样本更逼真且可控性更强。

Conclusion: X-VAE 通过数据自适应的精确先验和隐变量缩放机制，有效解决了 VAE 先验失配问题，在需要严格约束的工业与工程设计等应用中具有独特优势。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) commonly assume a standard isotropic Gaussian prior over the latent space, an assumption that often fails to capture the true distribution of latent representations for complex datasets. This mismatch can limit reconstruction accuracy, reduce sample quality, and constrain the expressive power of the learned latent space. We propose the eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE), a framework that replaces the conventional standard normal prior with a Gaussian prior derived from the latent representations of a pretrained autoencoder (AE). Specifically, the empirical mean and standard deviation of the AE latent codes are used to parameterize a data-adaptive prior that more closely reflects the underlying structure of the training data. During generation, X-VAE introduces a latent scaling factor that enables explicit control over the variance of the sampled latent vectors, providing a simple mechanism for balancing sample diversity and fidelity. This flexibility makes the proposed approach particularly well suited for applications such as industrial and engineering design, where generated solutions must satisfy strict structural or functional constraints while still permitting meaningful design exploration. We present the mathematical formulation of well-suited X-VAE, derive the corresponding KL divergence objective for the proposed prior, and evaluate the method on standard benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that X-VAE preserves reconstruction quality while producing latent representations that better align with the empirical data distribution, leading to improved controllability and more realistic generated samples.

</details>


### [300] [Full Bayesian Reinforcement Learning via LF-IBIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01741)
*Stefano Masini,Cecilia Viscardi,Michela Baccini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LF-IBIS 是一种无需似然函数的贝叶斯强化学习算法，结合近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）与迭代批量重要性采样（IBIS），在环境动态缺乏显式或可处理的似然函数时，实现在线信念更新，并输出环境参数与最优策略的后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数贝叶斯强化学习方法依赖显式似然函数，但现实场景中似然函数往往不可获取或不可处理。因此需要一种无需似然函数的 BRL 方法，既能在线更新信念，又能量化策略不确定性以指导探索-利用权衡。

Method: 提出 LF-IBIS 算法，将近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）与迭代批量重要性采样（IBIS）相结合。随着智能体与环境交互数据的逐步获取，在线更新环境参数和最优策略的近似后验分布，无需显式似然函数即可实现完整贝叶斯推断。

Result: 在临床试验响应自适应随机化的仿真研究中，利用具有闭合形式后验的场景验证了方法的有效性；在无闭合形式后验的场景中，展示了基于最优策略后验分布在线的策略更新能力。

Conclusion: LF-IBIS 成功突破了 BRL 对显式似然函数的依赖，能够在更广泛的实际问题中实现贝叶斯推断与策略不确定性量化，为数据稀缺环境下的在线决策提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a sequential decision-making framework in which an agent learns optimal policies through interaction with an environment by maximizing cumulative rewards. Among RL methods, Bayesian Reinforcement Learning (BRL) addresses common practical challenges related to data scarcity by leveraging prior knowledge about the environment and sequential belief updates. However, most BRL approaches require an explicit likelihood function, which is frequently inaccessible or intractable in real-world settings.
  We propose Likelihood-Free Iterated Batch Importance Sampling (LF-IBIS), a novel algorithm for BRL that updates the agent's beliefs online as new interactions become available. By combining Approximate Bayesian Computation with Iterated Batch Importance Sampling, LF-IBIS enables full Bayesian inference in settings where the environment dynamics are not described by an explicit or tractable likelihood. The method yields approximate posterior distributions over both environment parameters and optimal policies, providing a quantification of policy uncertainty useful for a Bayesian treatment of the exploration-exploitation trade-off. We test the method on a simulation study in response-adaptive randomization in clinical trials, where closed-form posteriors enable validation. Additional experiments address settings where the posterior has no closed form and illustrate online policy updating based on the posterior distribution of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [301] [Statistical Properties of $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Completely at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01945)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质，建立了聚类中心估计的√n收敛速率和渐近正态性，并给出了不完全数据估计收敛到完全数据真实中心的充分条件，为缺失数据k-means提供了理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于k-means的缺失数据聚类方法大多聚焦于提升实际聚类精度，但普遍缺乏渐近意义上的理论保证。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，系统研究缺失数据情形下k-means聚类的统计性质。

Method: 在一般缺失机制下建立√n超额风险界并证明聚类中心估计的一致性；针对完全随机缺失（MCAR）机制，进一步推导聚类中心估计的√n收敛速率和渐近正态性；研究不完全数据估计收敛到完全数据真实聚类中心的条件，给出关于缺失概率和真实簇间分离度的充分条件。

Result: 1）一般缺失机制下，证明了√n超额风险界和聚类中心的一致性；2）MCAR下，获得了√n收敛速率和渐近正态性；3）揭示了一个关键发现：在MCAR下，要达到√n速率并收敛到真实中心，需要k个真实中心在每一维度上都互异，这凸显了高维场景下应用的重大挑战。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失数据k-means聚类提供了严格的理论基础，揭示了MCAR机制下高维应用的根本性困难——要求各簇中心在所有维度上均相互分离。数值模拟验证了理论分析结果。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering cannot be directly used to incomplete data, and existing $k$-means-based clustering for missing data primarily focus on improving the practical accuracy of clustering, whereas most of them lack theoretical guarantees in the asymptotic sense. In this paper, we investigate the statistical properties of $k$-means clustering in the presence of missing data. We first establish the $\sqrt{n}$-excess risk bound and prove the consistency of the estimated cluster centers under general missing mechanisms. For the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR) mechanism, we further derive the $\sqrt{n}$-convergence rate and asymptotic normality of the estimated cluster centers. Moreover, we study in what cases the cluster centers estimated by incomplete data converge to the true cluster centers of original fully observed data, and give a sufficient condition about the missing probability and the separation among true clusters. These results provide a theoretical guarantee for missing-data-$k$-means. Notably, our analysis reveal that under MCAR mechanism, both achieving the $\sqrt{n}$-rate and converging to the true cluster centers require $k$ true centers to be distinct in every dimension, highlighting the significant challenges of application in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we conduct numerical simulations on synthetic incomplete datasets to support our theoretical analysis results.

</details>


### [302] [Autorelevance function and other feature relevance measures for univariate time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01959)
*Julian Cardenas,Jamie Arjona,Pedro Delicado*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关的滞后相关性度量方法，结合影子变量、Shapley值与加性重要性度量，引入自相关性和偏自相关性函数作为滞后重要性值，并提出用模型单步预测替代缺失特征的联盟方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单变量时间序列预测中，缺乏一种模型无关的方法来量化不同滞后阶数对预测的相对重要性，需要填补这一方法论空白。

Method: 结合影子变量（Ghost variables）框架与Shapley值，构建加性重要性度量；提出自相关性（auto-relevance）和偏自相关性（partial auto-relevance）函数用于量化滞后重要性；设计了一种用模型自身单步预测替代联盟法中缺失特征的新方法；在季节性ARMA族模型和循环神经网络上进行评估。

Result: 在模拟数据和真实数据上，所计算的滞后相关性度量几乎在所有情况下都能成功揭示预期的滞后结构。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效、模型无关地量化时间序列中滞后变量的重要性，为理解和解释预测模型中的滞后结构提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We propose a model agnostic methodology to measure lag relevance in machine learning forecasting models applied to univariate time series. Particularly, we are working in the context of time series using the frameworks of Ghost variables and Shapley values, together with additive importance measures, to introduce the auto-relevance and partial auto-relevance functions as the lag importance values. Additionally, we propose a novel method to replace absent features in coalition based methods with a one step forecast from the same model. We evaluate these proposals under different simulations and real data cases. This combined framework perspective is particularly suitable for time series. In addition, to show our discoveries we use a pull of models from the seasonal ARMA family and recurrent neural networks. We found that the calculated relevance measures successfully demonstrate the expected lag structure in almost all cases.

</details>


### [303] [Born Discrete, Made Smooth: Variational Formulation of Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02003)
*Matej Benko,Pierre Bousquet,Iwona Chlebicka,Błażej Miasojedow*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题转化为连续变分代理问题，在加权Sobolev空间中构造了一族λ-凸泛函，证明其全局适定、稳定且具有几乎C³正则性。该方法通过求解单个线性系统即可获得最优参数密度，完全绕开迭代优化，并给出了泛化误差的显式控制及有限宽度网络的O(1/N)收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经网络的优化原理在理论上仍不清晰，非凸景观和随机启发式方法使得训练过程的数学理解困难。现有的Wasserstein或平均场方法面临正则性不足和离散化挑战。作者旨在通过变分微积分建立一套更严谨、可分析的框架，弥合NTK与特征学习范式之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题重新表述为参数密度上的连续变分问题。在加权Sobolev空间中定义一族λ-凸泛函，利用椭圆正则性理论和凸分析工具，证明该变分问题全局适定且具有几乎C³正则性。最终证明最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得，无需迭代优化。

Result: （1）变分问题全局适定、稳定，解具有几乎C³正则性；（2）最优参数密度通过单个线性系统求解，绕过迭代优化；（3）泛化误差相对于正则化参数α以1/α速率受控；（4）有限宽度N的网络以O(1/N)速率逼近连续最优解。

Conclusion: 该工作通过变分微积分视角为过参数化提供了原则性框架，成功在NTK机制与特征学习机制之间架起桥梁，为理解神经网络优化本质开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Although neural networks are remarkably effective, their underlying optimization principles remain theoretically elusive, often characterized by non-convex landscapes and stochastic heuristics. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by replacing the discrete training problem of shallow neural networks with a well-posed continuum variational surrogate. We identify a family of $λ$-convex functionals over parameter densities in weighted Sobolev spaces and prove that these variational problems are globally well-posed, stable, and exhibit unexpected almost $C^3$ regularity.
  Unlike existing Wasserstein-based or Mean-Field approaches, which often face limited regularity and discretization challenges, our formulation provides direct access to elliptic regularity and convex analysis. This allows us to prove that the optimal parameter density can be obtained by solving a single linear system, bypassing iterative optimization entirely. We establish explicit generalization error controls at a rate of $1/α$ relative to the regularization parameter, and prove that finite-width networks of size $N$ achieve the continuum optimum at an $O(1/N)$ rate. This perspective bridges the gap between the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) and feature-learning regimes, providing a principled framework for understanding over-parameterization through the lens of variational calculus.

</details>


### [304] [Prediction Sets for Counterfactual Decisions: Coverage, Optimality, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02206)
*Yurui Zheng,Ying Jin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对反事实决策场景，提出了"策略耦合覆盖"这一新概念，并构建了决策论框架。该框架证明策略耦合覆盖是连接不确定性与行动的最优接口，等价于max-min规则下的极小化极大最优性，并开发了PC-RACP算法，在保证有限样本覆盖有效性的同时实现更高决策效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测被广泛用于高 stakes 决策，但现有不确定性量化方法（如共形预测）仅保证统计有效性，并未直接指导具体决策或优化决策效用。尤其在反事实设定下，结果取决于所采取的行动，因此不确定性不能独立于决策规则来定义。这造成了一个关键空白：如何在反事实决策中将预测不确定性转化为最优行动。

Method: 作者开发了一个不确定性驱动的反事实决策理论框架。核心创新是提出"策略耦合覆盖"——即预测集自身所诱导行动下实现结果的覆盖。作者证明：(1) 在分布模糊下，基于该覆盖的自然max-min规则是极小化极大最优的；(2) 在策略耦合覆盖下优化预测集，等价于更强的通用覆盖公式，也等价于对策略和效用证书的直接风险规避优化，从而得到总体最优预测集的显式形式；(3) 提出PC-RACP两阶段算法，在有限样本下近似最优集并保证覆盖有效性。

Result: 仿真实验和真实电子邮件营销实验均表明，PC-RACP相比现有方法能实现更高的效用，同时维持有效的覆盖保证；此外，忽略决策问题反事实结构的方法在有效性和效用方面均表现次优。

Conclusion: 策略耦合覆盖是连接预测不确定性与反事实决策的最优、无损失接口。PC-RACP框架为高 stakes 反事实决策提供了兼具理论保证与实用效用的解决方案。

Abstract: Predictions are increasingly used to guide high-stakes decisions, from treatment selection to policy making. To ensure reliability with imperfect predictions, uncertainty quantification methods such as conformal prediction build prediction sets with coverage guarantees. However, statistical validity alone does not immediately determine the decisions to take, nor the optimality thereof. This gap is especially delicate in counterfactual settings where the outcome that materializes depends on the action taken, so uncertainty cannot be specified independently of the decision rule.
  We develop a decision-theoretic framework for uncertainty-informed counterfactual decisions. We identify a novel notion of \emph{policy-coupled coverage} -- namely, coverage of the realized outcome under the action induced by the prediction sets themselves -- as the optimal and lossless interface between uncertainty and action. It plays three roles. First, it justifies acting via a natural max-min rule as minimax-optimal under distributional ambiguity. Second, optimizing prediction sets under policy-coupled coverage is equivalent both to a stronger universal-coverage formulation and to the direct risk-averse optimization over policies and utility certificates; this equivalence yields the explicit form of the population-optimal prediction sets. Third, it admits a two-stage procedure, Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction (PC-RACP), that approximates these optimal sets with rigorous finite-sample coverage. Simulations and a real email-marketing experiment confirm that PC-RACP delivers higher utility than existing approaches while maintaining valid coverage, and that ignoring the counterfactual structure of the decision problem is suboptimal for both validity and utility.

</details>


### [305] [An Additive MLP-GNN Framework for Characterizing Chemical and Structural Contributions to Aqueous Solubility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02212)
*Sampreeti Bhattacharya,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种加性深度学习框架，将物理化学描述符（化学分支）与分子图拓扑（结构分支）分离编码，仅在预测阶段通过加性模型（可选乘性交互）组合，从而实现对化学和结构贡献的显式分解与解释。结合预训练-微调策略，在AqSolDB和BigSolDB2数据集上取得竞争性预测性能，并通过投影、嵌入汇总和GNNExplainer分析验证了两个分支的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水溶性预测模型大多将物理化学描述符与分子图信息融合为单一表示，导致无法区分预测是由全局化学性质驱动、分子结构驱动、还是两者共同驱动，限制了模型的可解释性和透明度。

Method: 提出加性深度学习框架：(1) 化学分支：多层感知机（MLP）编码物理化学描述符；(2) 结构分支：图神经网络（GNN）编码分子图拓扑；(3) 两分支输出仅通过加性模型（含可选乘性交互项）在预测阶段组合，实现化学与结构成分的直接分解。训练策略上，先在较大的AqSolDB数据集上预训练，再在较小的BigSolDB2数据集上微调。事后解释方法包括：分支输出的最佳线性投影、不同溶解度类别的分子级嵌入汇总、以及基于功能团聚合的原子级GNNExplainer掩码。

Result: 预训练-微调策略显著提高了预测精度并降低了多次运行的方差，表明从数据丰富场景中学到的特征具有可迁移性。可解释性分析显示：化学分支与熟悉的物理化学描述符对齐良好；结构分支捕获了与溶解度相关的图拓扑和功能团模式。框架在两个数据集上均取得竞争性预测性能，同时使化学与结构信息的各自角色更加透明。

Conclusion: 该加性框架在不牺牲预测精度的前提下，成功实现了水溶性预测中化学因素与结构因素的解耦，为药物发现早期阶段的分子性质预测提供了更透明、可解释的建模范式。

Abstract: Aqueous solubility is a key property in early-stage drug discovery, but most predictive models merge physicochemical descriptors and molecular graph information into a single representation, obscuring whether a prediction is driven by global chemistry, molecular structure, or both. We present an additive deep-learning framework that keeps these two sources of information separate throughout training: physicochemical descriptors are encoded by a multilayer perceptron (the chemical branch) and molecular graph topology by a graph neural network (the structural branch), with the two outputs combined only at the prediction stage through an additive model with an optional multiplicative interaction. This design provides a direct decomposition of chemical and structural components that can be examined separately after training. Furthermore, pretraining on the larger AqSolDB dataset and fine-tuning on the smaller BigSolDB2 dataset substantially improve accuracy and reduce run-to-run variations, indicating generalizability of the learned features from the data-rich settings. We further interpret the fitted model using best linear projections of the branch outputs, molecule-level embedding summaries across solubility classes, and atom-level GNNExplainer masks aggregated over functional groups. These analyses show that the chemical branch aligns with familiar physicochemical descriptors, while the structural branch captures graph-topological and functional-group patterns associated with solubility. Across both datasets, the framework attains competitive predictive performance while making the distinct roles of chemical and structural information more transparent.

</details>


### [306] [The Dual Nature of LLM Persona: Aggregated Tendencies and Frame-Dependent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368)
*Yuan Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM人格表达包含两个可分离的成分：聚合特征（大五人格分数）对框架稳健但对随机化敏感，几何特征（SPD流形上的相关性结构）则高度依赖框架对齐，揭示了LLM人格的双重性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统心理测量问卷评估LLM人格仅依赖聚合分数，丢弃了项目间的相关结构信息。作者质疑这种几何结构是内在固有的还是框架依赖的，需要检验人格几何信息的本质。

Method: 使用IPIP-50问卷，在GPT-4o上模拟美国人和华裔美国人两种人格；通过操控问题顺序构建不同框架条件，在SPD流形上分析项目间相关矩阵的几何结构，对比聚合特征与几何特征在不同框架条件下的表现。

Result: 聚合特征（大五分数）在随机化下下降21%但对框架稳健；几何特征在框架错位下崩溃（下降42%），但在共享框架下大幅恢复至84%，超过聚合特征的76%恢复水平。

Conclusion: LLM人格呈现双重性质：框架依赖的几何结构与框架稳健的聚合特征。人格几何并非内在固有，而是一种框架依赖的协调模式，编码了聚合方法不可见的信息。这要求采用框架感知的评估方法，并对静态特质观念构成挑战。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM personas via psychometric questionnaires typically rely on aggregate scores, discarding within-instance correlation structure. We test whether this geometric structure is intrinsic or frame-dependent. Constructing within-instance correlation matrices from IPIP-50 responses, we analyze geometry on SPD manifolds under manipulated question orderings in GPT-4o simulating American and Chinese-American personas. We find that persona expression comprises two dissociable components: aggregated features (Big Five scores) degrade under randomization (21% drop) but are frame-robust; geometric features (SPD manifold) collapse under frame misalignment (42% drop) but recover substantially (to 84%) under shared frames, surpassing aggregated features (76%). This collapse-recovery pattern reveals that persona geometry is not intrinsic but a frame-dependent coordination pattern encoding information invisible to aggregation. Our findings establish a dual-nature framework for LLM personas, frame-dependent geometry versus frame-robust aggregates, necessitating frame-aware evaluation and challenging static trait conceptions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [307] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE 是一个模块化的神经符号框架，通过引入符号推理层对反事实生成过程施加领域约束，从而产生既有效又可行的反事实解释，解决了现有方法缺乏显式可行性机制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成改变预测的替代方案，但因缺乏显式的领域知识和干预约束机制，常产生不现实或不可行的建议。需要一种能够将数据驱动预测与符号推理相结合的方法，以生成符合领域约束的可操作解释。

Method: 提出 PACE 框架，将预测与推理分为两个组件：用于分类的神经预测模型（多层感知机），以及使用 Answer Set Programming (ASP) 规则编码可行修改（如教育、职业、工时）并保留不可变属性的符号推理层。该框架具有模型无关性和领域适应性。

Result: 在 Adult Income 数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束能在反事实有效性与合理性之间取得平衡，生成的解释能更好地满足领域特定的可行性要求。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在生成透明、可行性感知的反事实解释方面展现出显著潜力，为可解释 AI 中需要现实决策支持的领域提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [308] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个受约束的编码代理工作流，用于联邦学习算法配方的自动搜索。在多个医疗和LEAF数据集上评估，揭示了代理生成的候选方案可分解为可复现的FL机制、固定搜索面的调优效应和单次运行伪影三类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究中存在大量微小但关键的算法选择（优化器变体、服务器聚合规则、本地训练调度、归一化、正则化、模型架构等），手动探索成本高昂，且当候选变更同时改变训练或评估路径时难以公平比较。

Method: 提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个受约束的编码代理工作流。代理可提议并实现候选训练算法（包括服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标和已注册模型变体），任务配置文件则固定变异表面、计算预算、通信合约和最终模型评估。每个搜索活动记录候选分数、运行时间、编辑文件、产物和失败状态。

Result: 在五个医疗跨机构FLamby任务和五个固定LEAF数据集加LEAF合成任务的分组客户端配置上评估。五次种子重复评估显示：四个FLamby任务和六个LEAF配置中的五个获得增益，但也暴露了种子敏感和搜索选择的失败案例。同预算对照实验表明，部分增益归因于FL配方变更，其他改进则来自固定搜索面的标量调优或在重复/留出评估下失效。

Conclusion: 这些混合结果是贡献的一部分：展示了代理生成的候选方案如何被分解为可复现的FL机制、固定搜索面调优效应以及被选择的单次运行伪影，为联邦学习自动化算法搜索提供了系统性的分析框架。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [309] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola 是一个从零开始全新设计的小语言模型（SLM）架构，不继承任何现有模型家族的结构。它引入了五个独立创新组件：螺旋旋转位置编码（SRPE）、门控跨层注意力（GCLA）、自适应令牌合并（ATM）、双流前馈网络（DSFF）和带偏移向量的 RMSNorm 变体（WiolaRMSNorm）。模型提供四种规模，兼容 HuggingFace 生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流语言模型（GPT、LLaMA、Mistral 等）在架构上存在趋同现象，大多基于相似的 Transformer 变体。作者旨在从第一性原理出发，设计一个完全原创、不共享任何结构谱系的小语言模型架构，通过多个独立创新组件来解决位置编码、跨层信息流动、注意力复杂度、前馈网络表达能力和归一化稳定性等核心问题。

Method: Wiola 包含五项独立创新：（1）Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE)：将 token 位置嵌入三维螺旋流形，融合绝对、相对和层级位置信号；（2）Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA)：每个解码层通过软交叉注意力访问前两层的压缩摘要，增强跨层一致性；（3）Adaptive Token Merging (ATM)：在中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻 token，降低注意力复杂度；（4）Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF)：用双并行流替代传统 MLP，通过学习的每维度门控融合；（5）WiolaRMSNorm：在 RMSNorm 基础上引入每维度可学习的偏移向量，防止表示坍塌。论文提供了完整的数学推导、架构图、复杂度分析，并与 GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral 进行系统对比。

Result: Wiola 在四种规模（120M、360M、700M、1.5B 参数）上发布，完全兼容 HuggingFace Transformers 生态系统，全部 22 个架构单元测试通过。

Conclusion: Wiola 证明了从第一性原理出发构建完全原创的 SLM 架构是可行的，其五项创新组件在理论推导和工程实现上均得到验证，为语言模型架构的多元化探索开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [310] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agent4cs 是一个多智能体框架，采用自底向上的方式对大型代码库进行摘要，通过摘要智能体、关键词提取智能体和质量保证智能体的协同工作，在语义一致性和关键词覆盖率上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方案通常将源代码视为扁平文本，依赖单一语言模型，未能充分利用代码仓库中的层次结构和依赖关系，尤其在面对混淆结构和文档不完整的大型代码库时效果不佳。

Method: 提出 Agent4cs 多智能体框架，采用自底向上策略：摘要智能体负责生成鲁棒的代码摘要，关键词提取智能体从子文件夹中主动识别关键信息，质量保证智能体迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上评估，Agent4cs 相比两种结构化提示基线在所有文件夹层级上语义一致性平均提升8%；在真实数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。

Conclusion: Agent4cs 通过多智能体自底向上的协作机制，有效利用了代码仓库的结构化信息，在大型代码库摘要任务上取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [311] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规客服会话保持在低成本路径上，而将存在操作耦合的会话升级至包含冲突感知通信和写操作触发式反思的工作流中，从而在高风险后端写入前集中进行审慎控制，而非对所有会话均匀施加额外控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服代理正从对话界面转向操作执行角色（如退款、取消、订单修改等），这带来了服务控制问题：企业既要保持常规服务的快速低摩擦，又要防止客户指令、策略约束、企业记录和后端写入交互时产生操作错误。现有方案缺乏对高风险操作的精准控制。

Method: 提出一种难度路由服务控制架构（difficulty-routed service-control architecture）。包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级路由器，判断会话应走低成本基线路径还是升级路径；(2) 升级路径采用冲突感知通信（conflict-aware communication）和写操作触发式反思（write-triggered reconsideration），在关键后端写入前集中进行审慎推理和防护。

Result: 在τ²-bench的零售和航空任务上进行了人工验证评估。零售场景中，该方法在处理存在操作冲突的服务请求时持续提升可靠性。路由证据表明更强的控制被精准导向冲突请求而非广泛施加于常规请求。对话和工具使用分析显示，性能提升并非来自无差别的交互扩展或更广泛的工具链，而是来自新增的轮次和工具调用用于证据收集、写入分离和写前反思。案例层面证据表明升级工作流能保留备选方案、将检索记录绑定到正确操作、排序写入操作、并分解多实体请求。航空场景的结果将相同的服务控制逻辑扩展到了预订操作。

Conclusion: 难度路由服务控制架构能有效在保持常规服务效率的同时，精准对高风险操作耦合会话施加额外控制，显著提升操作可靠性。该方法的有效性在零售和航空两个领域均得到验证，表明其具有良好的领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [312] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativityNeuro是一种无需数据的对比权重引导方法，通过权重空间（而非激活空间）的操控来增强大语言模型的发散思维能力，在DAT、AUT和Task Task等多个创造力测试中显著提升了原创性、惊喜度和创造力，同时有效降低了模式坍塌现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在开放性问题中倾向于生成相似回复，即"人工蜂巢思维效应"（artificial hivemind effect），表现为模式坍塌（mode collapse），严重制约了LLM在创造性任务中的发散思维能力。现有方法通常依赖行为数据、重新训练或基于梯度的微调，成本高昂且泛化性有限，因此需要一种无需数据、可泛化到未见任务的方法来增强发散思维。

Method: 提出CreativityNeuro，一种基于对比权重引导（contrastive weight steering）的无数据方法。该方法在权重空间中直接对模型参数进行引导，而非在激活空间中进行操控，以此增强LLM的发散思维能力。通过对比机制调整权重方向，使模型倾向于生成更多样化、更具创造性的输出。

Result: 在发散联想任务（DAT）上，CreativityNeuro将表现提升了多达14个人类百分位数点。在大规模人类评估（N=720）中，该方法在替代用途测试（AUT）和Task Task上均实现了原创性、惊喜度和创造力的显著提升，并能迁移到更长篇幅、更开放的任务。在所有三项任务中，CreativityNeuro均显著降低了模式坍塌指标。此外，激活引导（activation steering）在DAT上表现相当，但无法迁移到AUT和Task Task，证明了权重空间引导在泛化到未见任务方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro能够在不依赖行为数据、无需重新训练或基于梯度微调的前提下，有效增强LLM的发散思维能力并减少模式坍塌，为提升LLM在创造性领域的表现提供了一种简洁直接的途径。权重空间引导相比激活空间引导具有更好的任务泛化性。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [313] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文适配了一个29B参数的混合专家扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B，在医学视觉问答任务上对其进行微调，结果表明扩散模型在性能上匹敌甚至超越同规模的自回归模型，同时具备自回归模型所缺乏的任意顺序文本填充能力，且在解码速度上快3.5-4.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学基础模型几乎全部采用自回归架构，而扩散语言模型在文本生成领域已展现出竞争力。作者旨在探索扩散语言模型在医学领域的适用性，特别是其双向去噪机制天然支持的任意顺序填充能力，能否更好地适配真实临床报告中因不同临床医生和机构导致的简略或不一致的文本。

Method: 采用混合专家（MoE）扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B，在相同的LoRA微调方案下，与其同规模自回归模型Gemma-4-26B在多个医学视觉问答数据集上进行对比评测，评分由对冗长性鲁棒的LLM裁判完成。

Result: 扩散模型在所有数据集上匹配或超越自回归模型；微调后的模型（3.8B活跃参数）性能可与前沿视觉-语言模型竞争；解码速度比自回归模型快3.5-4.4倍；此外，扩散模型展现出独有的任意顺序填充能力：放射科医生可修复报告片段，模型自动填充中间文本。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学领域不仅性能不逊于自回归模型，而且在解码效率和编辑灵活性方面具有显著优势，尤其是任意顺序填充能力非常适合真实临床报告场景，为医学基础模型提供了自回归之外的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [314] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在大语言模型应用于企业SaaS API调用场景时，由于训练目标（预测下一token）与实际任务（按特定顺序调用正确API端点并填充嵌套参数）之间的错位导致的静默失败问题。作者提出通过**可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）** 直接在目标环境中训练模型以弥合这一差距。实验基于模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API的五个合成环境，使用工具调用轨迹自动计算奖励（无人工标注、无裁判模型）。在Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B上的GRPO训练表明，RL策略将平均奖励从基线的0.35–0.92提升至0.95–1.00，其中Confluence页面创建任务提升最大（0.35→1.00）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的核心训练目标是下一token预测，而非在特定API中执行操作。在细分的SaaS企业工作流中，成功意味着按正确顺序调用正确端点并传入正确嵌套参数。这种目标错位表现为静默失败：遗漏必需字段、幻觉出不存在工具、或单次读取后提前停止。现有方法依赖人工标注或裁判模型，成本高且难以规模化。作者希望探索是否可以通过RLVR直接在目标环境中训练来弥合这一差距，实现面向结果优化的小模型。

Method: 构建五个高保真合成环境，模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API的schema约束；奖励信号完全从工具调用轨迹中自动计算（无实时API、无学习裁判、无人工标注）；使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）对Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B进行RLVR训练；以提示版本的4B模型作为基线，在相同的检查器上评分对比。

Result: 在四个奖励非退化的场景中，RL训练策略将4B基线的平均奖励从0.35–0.92提升至0.95–1.00。最大单次增益出现在Confluence页面创建任务（从0.35提升至1.00）。但五个场景中有一个（ticket-transition）的奖励形状已饱和，提示版4B模型即已取得满分。

Conclusion: RLVR可有效弥合LLM在特定企业API上的目标错位问题，实现近乎完美的工具调用性能。这项工作定位为面向细分企业API的结果优化小模型的初步探索。作者同时指出两大局限：（1）手工设计可验证奖励无法规模化到少数端点之外；（2）部分场景的奖励信号已饱和，无法在RL训练中提供进一步改进空间。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap.
